# Compliance Chatbot

> **LangGraph-powered NL2SQL compliance workspace for banking term-deposit products.**
> A six-node LangGraph pipeline converts plain-English questions into safe, read-only SQLite queries — powered by DLH (on-prem) or IBM ICA LLM providers.

---

1. [Overview](#overview)
2. [The Six-Node Pipeline](#the-six-node-pipeline)

## Overview

Compliance Chatbot is a banking compliance assistant built with **LangGraph**. It accepts natural-language questions about term-deposit accounts and routes each request through a six-node agentic pipeline that:

1. **Understands** the user's intent
2. **Introspects** the live database schema
3. **Writes** a correct SQLite SELECT statement
4. **Reviews** the SQL for safety and correctness
5. **Validates** SQL at AST level with sqlglot (deep structural check)
6. **Executes** it read-only and returns structured results

```
"How many senior citizens are in the Thane branch?"
        │
        ▼
 Intent Checker  →  Schema Checker  →  SQL Writer (LLM)  →  SQL Review  →  SQL Validator  →  Executor
        │
        ▼
 {"record_count": 12}   +   stages trace   +   result summary
```

The system ships with **four front-end options**, all connecting to the same FastAPI backend:

| Front-end | File | How to run | Default port |
|---|---|---|---|
| FastAPI HTML UI | `index.html` served by FastAPI | `python main.py` | `8000` |
| Standalone Python UI | `run_ui.py` | `python run_ui.py` | `CDSW_APP_PORT` |
| Gradio UI | `gradio_app.py` | `python gradio_app.py` | `7860` |
| **Streamlit UI** | `compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py` | `streamlit run compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py` | `8501` |

---

## The Six-Node Pipeline

Every request to `POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate` passes through this compiled LangGraph `StateGraph`:

| # | Node | File | LLM? | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Intent Checker** | `src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py` | ✗ | 4 ordered checks: empty → disallowed action word (DELETE/DROP/INSERT…) → too short/meaningless → vague/ambiguous. Ambiguous check skipped when question has >3 meaningful tokens. Halts with red card (blocked) or blue card (clarify). No LLM call. |
| 2 | **Schema Checker** | `src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py` | ✗ | Scans `data/**/*.db`. Scores each database by keyword overlap. Reads live `PRAGMA table_info`. Builds Markdown schema context including 3 sample rows. |
| 3 | **SQL Writer** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py` | **✓ only** | Pure LLM path — no rule-based fast path. Builds a structured few-shot prompt with live schema context. Calls DLH or ICA LLM. Extracts SQL from `<begin_sql_response>…<end_sql_response>` tags. |
| 4 | **SQL Review** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py` | ✗ | Checks LLM-generated SQL for disallowed keywords — sets user-facing `error` field + retries writer. Requires SELECT + FROM. Checks referenced tables exist in DB. Retries writer up to 3× with specific feedback. |
| 4.5 | **SQL Validator** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py` | ✗ | sqlglot AST parse, SELECT-type gate, SQLite dialect canonicalisation. See [sqlglot-validation.md](docs/sqlglot-validation.md). |
| 5 | **Executor** | `src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py` | ✗ | Opens SQLite with `BEGIN DEFERRED` (read-only). `fetchmany(500)`. Unconditional rollback. Returns `result_rows`, `result_columns`, and a human-readable `result_summary`. |

Pipeline routing logic:

```
[START] → Intent Checker
              │
              ├─ disallowed action word in question → ClarifyNode → [END]  (RED card)
              ├─ vague / ambiguous question         → ClarifyNode → [END]  (BLUE card)
              │
              └─ intent_ok=True → Schema Checker → SQL Writer → SQL Review
                                                    ▲                │
                                                    │  disallowed kw in SQL (≤3×)
                                                    │     review failed (≤3×)
                                                    │                │ review passed
                                                    │                ▼
                                                    │         SQL Validator (Node 4.5)
                                                    │                │
                                                    │   sqlglot_valid=False (≤3× total)
                                                    │                │ sqlglot_valid=True
                                                    └────────────────┘
                                                                     │
                                                              Executor → [END]
```

---

## Features

| Feature | Description |
|---|---|
| **LangGraph agentic pipeline** | Six dedicated nodes with conditional edges — intent, schema, write, review, validate, execute |
| **Natural-language to SQL** | Ask plain-English compliance questions, receive structured results |
| **Disallowed keyword blocking (user question)** | Intent Checker (Check 2) detects DELETE/DROP/INSERT/UPDATE and similar words in the user's raw question — halts immediately with a **red blocked card**, no LLM call made |
| **Disallowed keyword blocking (LLM SQL)** | SQL Review checks LLM-generated SQL for the same keywords — sets user-facing `error` field, retries writer up to 3× with corrective feedback |
| **Clarification flow** | Vague or ambiguous questions show a **blue clarification card** with four example questions — ambiguous-pronoun check skipped for questions with >3 meaningful tokens |
| **Three-layer read-only enforcement** | Layer 0: Intent keyword block · Layer 1: SQL keyword review · Layer 2: `BEGIN DEFERRED`/rollback in executor |
| **Live schema introspection** | Schema Checker reads the actual SQLite file at query time; schema never drifts |
| **Sample rows in prompt** | 3 real data rows included in schema context to ground the LLM |
| **SQL review + retry loop** | Validates safety + schema correctness; retries SQL Writer up to 3× with specific feedback |
| **AST-level SQL validation** | sqlglot parse + SELECT-type gate + SQLite dialect canonicalisation |
| **Dual LLM providers** | DLH (on-prem OpenAI-compatible) and IBM ICA |
| **Multi-database routing** | SchemaAgent scores all `.db` files by keyword match — no restart needed |
| **Backend status LED** | Topbar LED polls `GET /health` every 8 s — green when live, blinking red when offline |
| **CSV download per result** | Each query result has a ↓ Download as CSV button |
| **`result_summary`** | Human-readable summary (scalar result / row count / empty) |
| **Structured stage trace** | `stages` array shows every completed node step |
| **Live stage pills in UI** | Node pills light up in the browser as each step completes |
| **Rotating log files** | `RotatingFileHandler` — 10 MB per file, 10 backups — Windows-safe |
| **Persisted UI preferences** | Sidebar width, collapsed state, active page, LLM config survive refresh |
| **Urbanist typeface** | All 18 weights self-hosted from `assets/fonts/` |

---

```
│   │   └── 2_Chat.py                #   Chat page
│   ├── components/

│   │   ├── sidebar.py               #   Dark nav sidebar

│   └── utils/

│       ├── storage.py               #   Config persistence + chat export
│       └── markdown.py              #   HTML helpers (stage pills, chat bubbles, tables)
│
├── src/
│   ├── main.py                      # FastAPI app, endpoints, lifespan
│   │
│   ├── nl2sql_agents/               # ← LangGraph 6-node agentic pipeline
│   │   ├── state.py                 #   AgentState TypedDict (shared graph state)
│   │   ├── intent.py                #   Node 1 — Intent Checker (4 checks, no LLM)
│   │   ├── schema.py                #   Node 2 — Schema Checker (live PRAGMA introspection, no LLM)
│   │   ├── sql_writer_agent.py      #   Node 3 — SQL Writer (★ only LLM call; pure LLM path)
│   │   ├── sql_review.py            #   Node 4 — SQL Review (safety + schema; retry loop ≤3×)
│   │   ├── sql_validator.py         #   Node 4.5 — SQL Validator (sqlglot AST; no LLM)
│   │   ├── executor.py              #   Node 5 — Executor (read-only SQLite; rollback)
│   │   └── graph.py                 #   StateGraph factory — wires nodes + conditional edges
│   │
│   ├── business_logic/
│   │   └── compliance_processor.py  # Orchestration bridge: invokes graph, maps state → API models
│   │
│   ├── services/
│   │   └── model_loader_service.py  # DLH / ICA provider lifecycle and inference
│   │
│   ├── models/
│   │   ├── schemas.py               # Pydantic request / response models
│   │   └── compliance_schema.py     # Table column descriptions + few-shot LLM prompts
│   │
│   ├── exceptions/
│   │   └── custom_exceptions.py     # Domain exception hierarchy
│   │
│   └── utils/
│       ├── logger.py                # Centralised logging factory
│       └── constants.py             # Shared constants (SQL delimiter tags, table name, defaults)
│
├── config/
│   ├── settings.py                  # Pydantic-settings AppSettings (loaded from .env)
│   └── logging.yaml                 # RotatingFileHandler — 10 MB, 10 backups, Windows-safe
│
├── data/
│   └── comp_db/
│       └── COMP_REPORTS.db          # Default compliance SQLite database (created by seeder)
│
├── assets/
│   └── fonts/                       # Urbanist typeface — all 18 weights (.ttf)
│
├── docs/
│
├── tests/

│
├── logs/                            # Rotating log files (auto-created, git-ignored)
├── requirements.txt
├── pyproject.toml
└── .env.example
```

## Quick Start

### 1 — Prerequisites

- Python ≥ 3.11
- Access to a DLH inference endpoint **or** an IBM ICA API key

### 2 — Install dependencies

```bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### 3 — Configure environment

```bash
cp .env.example .env
# Edit .env and set your LLM provider credentials
```

Minimum DLH configuration:

```env
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://your-dlh-host:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
```

### 4 — Seed the database

```bash
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
```

This creates `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` with 2,000 realistic synthetic banking rows. **This step is required before the first use.**

### 5 — Start the application

```bash
python main.py
# FastAPI starts on http://0.0.0.0:8000
# Open http://localhost:8000 in your browser
```

---

## How the Two `main.py` Files Work

There are intentionally **two** files named `main.py` in this project:

| File | Role | When to use |
|---|---|---|
| `launcher.py` | **Preferred entry-point** — starts FastAPI backend + standalone UI as sub-processes; watchdog monitors both | `python launcher.py` — starts everything together |
| `main.py` (project root) | **CLI entry-point** — parses `--host`, `--port`, `--reload` and launches uvicorn pointing at `src.main:app` | `python main.py` — backend only |
| `src/main.py` | **FastAPI application** — defines `app`, all API endpoints, lifespan handler, and static-file serving | Never run directly; referenced as `src.main:app` by uvicorn |

```
python launcher.py
    │
    ├─► python main.py   ──► uvicorn ──► src.main:app ──► FastAPI lifespan
    │                                                          │
    │                                                          ├─ ModelLoaderService.load()   ← DLH / ICA client
    │                                                          ├─ build_nl2sql_graph()        ← LangGraph compile
    │                                                          └─ ComplianceProcessor()
    │
    └─► python run_ui.py ──► HTTPServer (CDSW_APP_PORT)
```

To launch with uvicorn directly (e.g., production multi-worker):

```bash
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --workers 4
```

---

## Running the Application

### Option 0 — `python launcher.py` (recommended — starts everything)

```bash
python launcher.py
```

Starts both the FastAPI backend (`main.py`) and the standalone UI (`run_ui.py`) as sub-processes. A watchdog loop monitors both; restarts them if they crash. Set ports via `CDSW_APP_PORT` (UI) and `PORT` (backend) in `.env`.

### Option 1 — `python main.py` (backend only)

```bash
python main.py
```

Reads `HOST`, `PORT`, `DEBUG`, `LOG_LEVEL` from `.env`. Starts `src.main:app` via uvicorn. The HTML UI is served at `GET /`.

### Option 2 — uvicorn directly

```bash
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```

Use `--reload` for development. Use `--workers 4` for production.

### Option 3 — Module mode

```bash
python -m src.main
```

---

## UI Options

All four UIs connect to the same FastAPI backend.

### Option A — FastAPI HTML UI (default)

Served automatically at `http://localhost:8000` when the backend is running.

**Features:** Live LangGraph stage pills, result summary display, scrollable chat (480 px), Urbanist font, collapsible + resizable sidebar, mobile responsive, `localStorage` persistence.

### Option B — Standalone Python UI (`run_ui.py`)

```bash
python run_ui.py
# Opens http://localhost:<CDSW_APP_PORT> automatically
```

Zero extra dependencies (Python standard library only). Proxies all `/api/v1/*` and `GET /health` calls to the FastAPI backend. Includes backend status LED, CSV download per result, red blocked card, and blue clarification card.

| Environment variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CDSW_APP_PORT` | *(required)* | Port the UI server listens on |
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://0.0.0.0:8066` | FastAPI backend to proxy to |

### Option C — Gradio UI (`gradio_app.py`)

```bash
python gradio_app.py
# Opens http://localhost:7860 automatically
```

Requires `gradio>=4.36` (included in `requirements.txt`). Saves LLM config to `.cb_saved_config.json`.

| Environment variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CB_GRADIO_PORT` | `7860` | Gradio server port |
| `CB_BASE_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8000` | FastAPI backend URL |

### Option D — Streamlit UI (`compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py`) ⭐ Recommended

```bash
streamlit run compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py
# Opens http://localhost:8501 automatically
```

Production-quality Streamlit UI with dark sidebar, LLM Configuration page, and full Chat page.

**Features:** Dark sidebar (`#0d1526`), ICA/DLH provider toggle, stage pills (Intent Analysis → DB Selection → SQL Generation → Clarification → Execution), HTML chat viewport with user/bot bubbles, interactive `st.dataframe` + `st.code` tabs for last result, Copy / Export / Attach / New Chat / Send toolbar, backend health indicator, config persistence to `.cb_saved_config.json`.

| Environment variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8000` | FastAPI backend URL |

```bash
# Override backend URL
CB_BACKEND_URL=http://10.1.2.3:8000 streamlit run compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py
```

### UI Feature Comparison

| Feature | FastAPI HTML | Standalone Python (`run_ui.py`) | Gradio | **Streamlit** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| LLM Configuration form | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Test & Save (rebuilds LangGraph) | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Live LangGraph stage pills (6 nodes) | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Result summary display | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Agent trace in bot reply | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| **Backend status LED** | — | **Yes** | — | — |
| **CSV download per result** | — | **Yes** | — | — |
| **Red blocked card** | Yes | **Yes** | Yes | Yes |
| **Blue clarification card** | Yes | **Yes** | Yes | Yes |
| Scrollable chat viewport | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Copy / Export chat | Yes | Yes | Yes | **Yes** |
| Interactive data table | No | No | No | **Yes (st.dataframe)** |
| SQL code tab | No | No | No | **Yes (st.code)** |
| Dark sidebar | Yes | Yes | No | **Yes** |
| Config persistence | localStorage | localStorage | `.cb_saved_config.json` | **`.cb_saved_config.json`** |
| Auto-opens browser | No | **Yes** | **Yes** | **Yes** |
| Extra dependencies | fastapi, uvicorn | **None** | gradio | streamlit, pandas |

---

## Database Setup

The database is **not** auto-seeded. Run the seeder script before first use:

```bash
# Recommended first run — wipe and insert 2,000 rows
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000

# Append rows to existing data
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 1000

# Custom count with fixed random seed
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 5000 --seed 99
```

The seeder creates `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` and the `COMP_TBL` table if they do not exist. It uses only the Python standard library — no `pip install` required.

After seeding 2,000 rows the DB contains approximately:

| Metric | Count |
|---|---|
| Total rows | 2,000 |
| Senior citizens (`SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1`) | ~759 (38%) |
| KYC non-compliant (`KYC_FLG = 'N'`) | ~267 (13%) |
| Circles covered | 13 cities, 20 codes |
| Products | 11 term-deposit products |

> See [docs/synthetic-data.md](docs/synthetic-data.md) for full details.

---

## Configuration

All settings are loaded from environment variables or a `.env` file. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and edit.

### Application Settings

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `HOST` | `0.0.0.0` | FastAPI bind host |
| `PORT` | `8000` | FastAPI bind port |
| `DEBUG` | `false` | Enable uvicorn hot-reload |
| `LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level (`DEBUG`, `INFO`, `WARNING`, `ERROR`) |
| `LOG_DIR` | `logs` | Directory for rotating log files |

### Database Settings

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `DATABASE__DB_FOLDER` | `data/comp_db` | Folder containing `.db` files |
| `DATABASE__DB_NAME` | `COMP_REPORTS.db` | Default database filename |

### LLM Provider Settings

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `MODEL__PROVIDER` | `dlh` | Active provider: `dlh` or `ica` |
| `MODEL__MODEL_NAME` | `google/gemma-4-31b-it` | Model name or ICA resource ID |
| `MODEL__MODEL_MAX_NEW_TOKENS` | `512` | Max tokens to generate per call |
| `MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE` | `0.1` | Sampling temperature |
| `MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL` | `http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1` | DLH inference server base URL |
| `MODEL__DLH_API_KEY` | `EMPTY` | DLH bearer token |
| `MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL` | `false` | Verify DLH TLS certificate |
| `MODEL__ICA_BASE_URL` | `https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com` | ICA API host URL |
| `MODEL__ICA_API_KEY` | _(empty)_ | ICA developer API key |
| `MODEL__ICA_NAMESPACE` | `chat-models` | ICA namespace (`chat-models`, `agents`, `assistants`, `digital-workforce`) |
| `MODEL__ICA_SEND_TEMPERATURE` | `false` | Forward temperature/max_tokens to ICA |

> See [docs/configuration.md](docs/configuration.md) for the full reference including all `.env` examples and settings class documentation.

---

## API Endpoints

| Method | Path | Tags | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/` | — | Serve the HTML UI (`index.html`) |
| `GET` | `/health` | Infrastructure | Liveness probe — returns `{"status":"ok","version":"2.0.0"}` |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/config/llm` | Configuration | Configure and test an LLM provider; rebuilds the LangGraph pipeline |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/compliance/chat` | Compliance | NL2SQL chat — simplified response |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate` | Compliance | NL2SQL chat — full LangGraph stage trace + result columns |
| `GET` | `/docs` | — | Swagger / OpenAPI interactive UI |
| `GET` | `/redoc` | — | ReDoc interactive documentation |

### Request body (both compliance endpoints)

```json
{
  "question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?",
  "question_id": "req-001"
}
```

> See [docs/api-reference.md](docs/api-reference.md) for all request/response schemas, stage string reference, error formats, and curl examples.

---

## Sample API Response

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?"}'
```

```json
{
  "question_id": "7f3c9d1a-...",
  "interpreted_question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?",
  "needs_clarification": false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE UPPER(CIRCLENAME) LIKE '%MUMBAI%';",
  "df_output": [{"record_count": 306}],
  "result_columns": ["record_count"],
  "result_summary": "Result: **record_count** = `306`",
  "stages": [
    "intent_agent:ok",
    "schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db",
    "sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0",
    "sql_review_agent:passed",
    "sql_validator_agent:passed dialect=sqlite",
    "executor_agent:ok rows=1"
  ],
  "error": null
}
```

---

## Architecture Overview

```
Browser UI (index.html / run_ui.py / gradio_app.py)
        │
        │  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate
        ▼
  FastAPI  (src/main.py)
        │
        ▼
  ComplianceProcessor  (src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py)
        │  compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
        ▼
  LangGraph StateGraph  (src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py)
       │
       ├──► Node 1   Intent Checker  (intent.py)          — 4-check intent gate; no LLM
       ├──► Node 2   Schema Checker  (schema.py)          — live PRAGMA introspection + DB scoring
       ├──► Node 3   SQL Writer      (sql_writer_agent.py) — ★ only LLM call; pure LLM path
       ├──► Node 4   SQL Review      (sql_review.py)       — safety + schema validation (retry ≤3×)
       ├──► Node 4.5 SQL Validator   (sql_validator.py)    — sqlglot AST parse + canonicalise
       └──► Node 5   Executor        (executor.py)         — read-only execution + result summary
                    │
                    ▼
              SQLite  (data/**/*.db)
```

> See [docs/architecture.md](docs/architecture.md) for the full layered diagram, `AgentState` field ownership table, request-flow walkthrough, and security design.

---

## Database Schema

The default table `COMP_TBL` in `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` stores banking term-deposit compliance records.

| Column | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `BRCD` | TEXT | Branch code |
| `CUST_NBR` | TEXT | Customer number |
| `ACCT_NBR` | TEXT | Account number |
| `AGE_YRS` | INTEGER | Customer age in years |
| `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG` | INTEGER | Senior citizen flag (`1` = yes, `0` = no) |
| `KYC_FLG` | TEXT | KYC compliance status (`Y` = compliant, `N` = non-compliant) |
| `END_OF_DAY_BAL` | REAL | End-of-day account balance (INR) |
| `MTRTY_DT` | TEXT | Account maturity date (`YYYY-MM-DD`) |
| `NEW_EFFCTV_RT` | REAL | New effective interest rate (%) |
| `EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT` | REAL | Expected effective interest rate (%) |
| `PRODUCT_NAME` | TEXT | Term deposit product name |
| `BRANCHNAME` | TEXT | Full branch name |
| `CIRCLENAME` | TEXT | Circle / administrative region name |

> See [docs/database-schema.md](docs/database-schema.md) for the full 25-column reference with all types and descriptions.

---

## Testing

```bash
# Run all tests
pytest

# With verbose output
pytest -v

# With coverage report
pytest --cov=src --cov-report=term-missing

# Specific test file
pytest tests/test_intent.py -v
```

Write new tests in `tests/`. Each node is a plain Python class with a `.run(state) -> AgentState` method — only `sql_writer_agent.py` requires a mocked `ModelLoaderService`. All other nodes are fully deterministic.

| Module | Coverage target |
|---|---|
| `src/nl2sql_agents/` | ≥ 90% |
| `src/business_logic/` | ≥ 90% |
| `src/services/` | ≥ 85% |
| `src/models/` | ≥ 95% |
| `src/exceptions/` | ≥ 100% |

> See [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md) for full details, test patterns, and CI integration.

## Deployment

### Local development

```bash
python main.py
```

### Production (uvicorn, multi-worker)

```bash
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --workers 4 --log-level info
```

### Docker

```dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt
COPY . .
EXPOSE 8000
CMD ["uvicorn", "src.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--log-level", "info"]
```

```bash
docker build -t compliance-bot:latest .
docker run -d -p 8000:8000 \
  -e MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh \
  -e MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://your-dlh-host:9000/api/v1 \
  -v $(pwd)/data:/app/data \
  compliance-bot:latest
```





####################################################################################################
FILE: launcher.py
####################################################################################################

import subprocess
import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

def launch_compliance_bot():
    # --- 1. CONFIGURATION (Check these paths!) ---
    # Change 'main.py' and 'run_ui.py' if your filenames are different
    BACKEND_FILE = Path(r"C:\Users\BishwajitPrasadGond\Desktop\COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT\main.py") 
    UI_FILE = Path(r"C:\Users\BishwajitPrasadGond\Desktop\COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT\run_ui.py")
    
    BACKEND_PORT = "8001"
    UI_PORT = os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", "8100")

    print("\n Checking environment...")

    # Check if files exist first to avoid silent failures
    if not BACKEND_FILE.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: Backend file not found at {BACKEND_FILE}")
        return
    if not UI_FILE.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: UI file not found at {UI_FILE}")
        return

    # Setup Environment Variables
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"] = f"http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}"
    env["CDSW_APP_PORT"] = UI_PORT

    print("Starting Compliance Bot Suite...")

    try:
        # --- 2. START BACKEND ---
        print(f" Launching Backend (FastAPI) on port {BACKEND_PORT}...")
        # We use 'python -m uvicorn' for better compatibility with CML environments
        backend_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            ["python", "-m", "uvicorn", "src.main:app", "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", BACKEND_PORT],
            env=env
        )

        # IMPORTANT: Give the backend time to initialize LangGraph and the API
        print(" Waiting 7 seconds for Backend to stabilize...")
        time.sleep(7)

        # --- 3. START UI SERVER ---
        print(f" Launching UI Server on port {UI_PORT}...")
        ui_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            ["python", str(UI_FILE)],
            env=env
        )

        print("\n" + "="*60)
        print(" SYSTEMS STARTED")
        print(f"Backend Proxy: http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}")
        print(f"Frontend UI:   http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}")
        print("="*60)
        print("\n Use the CML-provided link for port 8100 to open the UI.")
        print(" Press Ctrl+C to stop both servers.\n")

        # Keep the main thread alive so the background processes keep running
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)
            # If either process crashes, let the user know
            if backend_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n BACKEND CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break
            if ui_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n UI SERVER CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n XXXXX------Shutting down servers-----XXXXX")
    finally:
        # Cleanly kill both processes
        if 'backend_proc' in locals():
            backend_proc.terminate()
        if 'ui_proc' in locals():
            ui_proc.terminate()
        print(" All processes stopped.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    launch_compliance_bot()






####################################################################################################
FILE: main.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Compliance Chatbot v2 — root project entry-point.

This file is the single command users run to start the application:

    python main.py                             # start on default host/port from .env
    python main.py --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8080 # override host / port
    python main.py --reload                    # enable hot-reload (dev only)

Relationship between this file and src/main.py
-----------------------------------------------
  • **main.py** (this file, project root) — CLI wrapper.
    Parses command-line arguments, reads AppSettings from config/settings.py,
    then launches uvicorn pointing at ``src.main:app``.

  • **src/main.py** (FastAPI application) — the actual ASGI application.
    Defines the FastAPI ``app`` instance, all HTTP endpoints, the lifespan
    handler (DB seed + LangGraph pipeline bootstrap), and static-file serving.
    Never run this file directly — always use the root ``main.py`` or uvicorn.

Environment variables (or .env file):
    HOST        Bind host          (default: 0.0.0.0)
    PORT        Bind port          (default: 8000)
    DEBUG       Enable hot-reload  (default: false)
    LOG_LEVEL   Uvicorn log level  (default: INFO)
"""

import argparse

import uvicorn

from config.settings import get_settings

def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    cfg = get_settings()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Start the Compliance Chatbot v2 FastAPI server. "
            "The LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline is initialised automatically on startup."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument("--host", default=cfg.host, help="Bind host.")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=cfg.port, help="Bind port.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--reload",
        action="store_true",
        default=cfg.debug,
        help="Enable uvicorn hot-reload (development only).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--log-level",
        default=cfg.log_level.lower(),
        help="Uvicorn log level.",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()

def main() -> None:
    """Parse CLI arguments and launch the FastAPI ASGI application via uvicorn."""
    args = _parse_args()
    uvicorn.run(
        "src.main:app",   # ← points at the FastAPI app defined in src/main.py
        host=args.host,
        port=args.port,
        reload=args.reload,
        log_level=args.log_level,
    )

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import json\n",
    "import requests\n",
    "import pandas as pd\n",
    "from IPython.display import display, Markdown, HTML\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── Low-level HTTP helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "\n",
    "def _post(path: str, payload: dict, timeout: int = 120) -> dict:\n",
    "    \"\"\"POST to the backend and return the parsed JSON body.\"\"\"\n",
    "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=timeout)\n",
    "        resp.raise_for_status()\n",
    "        return resp.json()\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": f\"Cannot connect to backend at {BACKEND_URL}. Is `python main.py` running?\"}\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as exc:\n",
    "        try:\n",
    "            body = exc.response.json()\n",
    "        except Exception:\n",
    "            body = {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "        return body\n",
    "    except Exception as exc:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "\n",
    "def _get(path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n",
    "    \"\"\"GET from the backend and return the parsed JSON body.\"\"\"\n",
    "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        resp = requests.get(url, timeout=timeout)\n",
    "        resp.raise_for_status()\n",
    "        return resp.json()\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": f\"Cannot connect to backend at {BACKEND_URL}.\"}\n",
    "    except Exception as exc:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── Display helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "\n",
    "def _print_stages(stages: list):\n",
    "    if not stages:\n",
    "        return\n",
    "    pills = \" → \".join(f\"`{s}`\" for s in stages)\n",
    "    display(Markdown(f\"**Agent stages:** {pills}\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "def _print_result(data: dict):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Pretty-print a /orchestrate response.\"\"\"\n",
    "    if data.get(\"error\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"❌ **Error:** {data['error']}\"))\n",
    "        return\n",
    "\n",
    "    _print_stages(data.get(\"stages\", []))\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"needs_clarification\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"❓ **Clarification needed:** {data.get('clarification_question')}\"))\n",
    "        return\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"sql_query\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Generated SQL**\\n```sql\\n{data['sql_query']}\\n```\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"result_summary\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Summary:** {data['result_summary']}\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "    rows = data.get(\"df_output\") or []\n",
    "    cols = data.get(\"result_columns\") or []\n",
    "    if rows:\n",
    "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=cols if cols else None)\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Rows returned:** {len(df)}\"))\n",
    "        display(df)\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        display(Markdown(\"*No rows returned.*\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"Helpers loaded ✔\")"
   ]
  },

   "cell_type": "markdown",

    "## Cell 7 — Interactive chat loop (ipywidgets)\n",
    "\n",
    "A lightweight widget-based chat. Type your question and press **Send** (or hit Enter).  \n",
    "Requires `ipywidgets` and the `widgetsnbextension` to be enabled.\n",
    "\n",
    "```bash\n",
    "jupyter nbextension enable --py widgetsnbextension\n",
    "```"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
    "from IPython.display import clear_output\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── UI components ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "question_box = widgets.Textarea(\n",
    "    placeholder=\"Type your compliance question here…\",\n",
    "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\", height=\"70px\"),\n",
    ")\n",
    "send_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
    "    description=\"Send\",\n",
    "    button_style=\"primary\",\n",
    "    tooltip=\"Send question to Compliance Bot\",\n",
    "    icon=\"paper-plane\",\n",
    ")\n",
    "clear_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
    "    description=\"Clear chat\",\n",
    "    button_style=\"warning\",\n",
    "    tooltip=\"Clear conversation history\",\n",
    "    icon=\"trash\",\n",
    ")\n",
    "out = widgets.Output()\n",
    "status_label = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
    "\n",
    "# Conversation history stored as list of (role, text, data) tuples\n",
    "_history: list = []\n",
    "\n",
    "def _render_history():\n",
    "    for role, text, data in _history:\n",
    "        if role == \"user\":\n",
    "            display(Markdown(f\"---\\n**You:** {text}\"))\n",
    "        else:\n",
    "            display(Markdown(\"**Bot:**\"))\n",
    "            _print_result(data)\n",
    "\n",
    "def on_send(btn):\n",
    "    question = question_box.value.strip()\n",
    "    if not question:\n",
    "        return\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"⏳ Waiting for response…\"\n",
    "    send_btn.disabled = True\n",
    "    question_box.value = \"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    data = _post(\"/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate\", {\"question\": question})\n",
    "    _history.append((\"user\", question, None))\n",
    "    _history.append((\"bot\", \"\", data))\n",
    "\n",
    "    with out:\n",
    "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
    "        _render_history()\n",
    "\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"✅ Done\"\n",
    "    send_btn.disabled = False\n",
    "\n",
    "def on_clear(btn):\n",
    "    _history.clear()\n",
    "    with out:\n",
    "        clear_output()\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"Chat cleared.\"\n",
    "\n",
    "send_btn.on_click(on_send)\n",
    "clear_btn.on_click(on_clear)\n",
    "\n",
    "# Layout\n",
    "btn_row = widgets.HBox([send_btn, clear_btn, status_label])\n",
    "ui = widgets.VBox([\n",
    "    widgets.HTML(\"<h3>💬 Compliance Bot Chat</h3>\"),\n",
    "    question_box,\n",
    "    btn_row,\n",
    "    out,\n",
    "])\n",
    "display(ui)"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "markdown",
   "metadata": {},
   "source": [
    "## Cell 8 — Batch queries from a list\n",

    "Run multiple questions at once and compare results side-by-side."
   ]

   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,

    "    \"How many accounts are maturing this year?\",\n",
    "]\n",

    "batch_results = []\n",
    "\n",
    "for i, q in enumerate(BATCH_QUESTIONS, 1):\n",
    "    display(Markdown(f\"---\\n### Question {i}: {q}\"))\n",
    "    data = _post(\"/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate\", {\"question\": q})\n",
    "    _print_result(data)\n",
    "    batch_results.append({\n",
    "        \"question\": q,\n",
    "        \"sql\": data.get(\"sql_query\", \"\"),\n",
    "        \"summary\": data.get(\"result_summary\", \"\"),\n",
    "        \"rows\": len(data.get(\"df_output\") or []),\n",
    "        \"error\": data.get(\"error\", \"\"),\n",
    "    })\n",
    "\n",
    "# Summary table\n",
    "display(Markdown(\"---\\n## Batch Summary\"))\n",
    "display(pd.DataFrame(batch_results)[[\"question\", \"rows\", \"summary\", \"error\"]])"
   ]
  },

    "\n",
    "| # | Question | Expected behaviour |\n",
    "|---|---|---|\n",
    "| 1 | `How many persons are from the Mumbai circle?` | COUNT query — rule-based fast path |\n",
    "| 2 | `List all senior citizen accounts` | SELECT with `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1` |\n",
    "| 3 | `Show KYC non-compliant accounts in Delhi` | Compound WHERE filter |\n",
    "| 4 | `What is the average balance for Thane branch?` | AVG aggregate |\n",
    "| 5 | `How many accounts are maturing next month?` | Date-range filter on `MTRTY_DT` |\n",
    "| 6 | `Top 10 accounts by end-of-day balance` | ORDER BY + LIMIT |\n",
    "| 7 | `Count accounts by product name` | GROUP BY aggregate |\n",
    "| 8 | `Who?` | Triggers clarification flow |\n",
    "\n",

    "- Re-run **Cell 5** to switch LLM providers without restarting the notebook.\n",

      "cell_type": "markdown",

      "source": [
        "# Compliance Bot Interactive Notebook\n",
        "\n",
        "This notebook provides an interactive interface for configuring the LLM provider and asking natural-language compliance questions against the Compliance Bot FastAPI backend.\n",
        "\n",
        "## Prerequisites\n",
        "\n",
        "Before running this notebook, ensure that the following steps have been completed:\n",
        "\n",
        "1. Start the FastAPI backend:\n",
        "\n",
        "   ```bash\n",
        "   python main.py\n",
        "   ```\n",
        "\n",
        "   By default, the backend runs at `http://127.0.0.1:8000`.\n",
        "\n",
        "2. Seed the database with synthetic data:\n",
        "\n",
        "   ```bash\n",
        "   python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000\n",
        "   ```\n",
        "\n",
        "---\n",
        "\n",
        "## Agent Pipeline\n",
        "\n",
        "The Compliance Bot processes each question through a multi-agent pipeline:\n",
        "\n",
        "```text\n",
        "User Question\n",
        "     |\n",
        "     v\n",
        "IntentAgent -> SchemaAgent -> SQLWriterAgent -> SQLReviewAgent -> ExecutorAgent\n",
        "     |\n",
        "     v\n",
        "Structured result, generated SQL, and execution trace\n",
        "```"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {},
      "source": [
        "## Cell 1: Install and Verify Dependencies"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": 2,
      "metadata": {},

          "name": "stdout",

            "pandas is already installed\n",
            "tabulate is already installed\n",

            "All dependencies are ready.\n"
          ]
        }
      ],
      "source": [
        "# Install required packages if they are not already available in the environment.\n",
        "import importlib\n",
        "import subprocess\n",
        "import sys\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _ensure(pkg, import_name=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Install a package only when its import target is unavailable.\"\"\"\n",
        "    name = import_name or pkg\n",
        "    if importlib.util.find_spec(name) is None:\n",
        "        print(f\"Installing {pkg}...\")\n",
        "        subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, \"-m\", \"pip\", \"install\", pkg, \"-q\"])\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(f\"{pkg} is already installed\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "_ensure(\"requests\")\n",
        "_ensure(\"ipywidgets\")\n",
        "_ensure(\"pandas\")\n",
        "_ensure(\"tabulate\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nAll dependencies are ready.\")"
      ]
    },

        "## Cell 3: Helper Utilities"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": 4,
      "metadata": {},
      "outputs": [
        {
          "name": "stdout",
          "output_type": "stream",
          "text": [
            "Helper utilities loaded successfully.\n"
          ]
        }
      ],
      "source": [
        "import json\n",
        "import requests\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "from IPython.display import display, Markdown\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# HTTP helper functions\n",
        "\n",
        "def _post(path: str, payload: dict, timeout: int = 120) -> dict:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Send a POST request to the backend and return the parsed JSON response.\"\"\"\n",
        "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        response = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=timeout)\n",
        "        response.raise_for_status()\n",
        "        return response.json()\n",
        "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"error\": f\"Cannot connect to the backend at {BACKEND_URL}. Confirm that `python main.py` is running.\"\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as exc:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return exc.response.json()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _get(path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Send a GET request to the backend and return the parsed JSON response.\"\"\"\n",
        "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        response = requests.get(url, timeout=timeout)\n",
        "        response.raise_for_status()\n",
        "        return response.json()\n",
        "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
        "        return {\"error\": f\"Cannot connect to the backend at {BACKEND_URL}.\"}\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Display helper functions\n",
        "\n",
        "def _print_stages(stages: list):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Display the list of agent stages executed by the pipeline.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if not stages:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    stage_text = \" -> \".join(f\"`{stage}`\" for stage in stages)\n",
        "    display(Markdown(f\"**Agent stages:** {stage_text}\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _print_result(data: dict):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Render a formatted response from the /orchestrate endpoint.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if data.get(\"error\"):\n",
        "        display(Markdown(f\"**Error:** {data['error']}\"))\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    _print_stages(data.get(\"stages\", []))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if data.get(\"needs_clarification\"):\n",
        "        display(Markdown(f\"**Clarification required:** {data.get('clarification_question')}\"))\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    if data.get(\"sql_query\"):\n",
        "        display(Markdown(f\"**Generated SQL**\\n```sql\\n{data['sql_query']}\\n```\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if data.get(\"result_summary\"):\n",
        "        display(Markdown(f\"**Summary:** {data['result_summary']}\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    rows = data.get(\"df_output\") or []\n",
        "    columns = data.get(\"result_columns\") or []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if rows:\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=columns if columns else None)\n",
        "        display(Markdown(f\"**Rows returned:** {len(df)}\"))\n",
        "        display(df)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        display(Markdown(\"No rows were returned.\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Helper utilities loaded successfully.\")"
      ]
    },
    {

        "## Cell 7: Interactive Chat Interface\n",
        "\n",
        "This cell provides a lightweight widget-based chat interface. Enter a compliance question and select **Send** to submit it to the backend.\n",
        "\n",
        "This feature requires `ipywidgets`. If the widgets do not render or the buttons are not responsive, enable the widget extension:\n",
        "\n",
        "```bash\n",
        "jupyter nbextension enable --py widgetsnbextension\n",
        "```"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": 8,
      "metadata": {},
      "outputs": [
        {
          "data": {
            "application/vnd.jupyter.widget-view+json": {
              "model_id": "67c4e7bfe1024e50ac6e86503649588f",
              "version_major": 2,
              "version_minor": 0
            },
            "text/plain": [
              "VBox(children=(HTML(value='<h3>Compliance Bot Chat</h3>'), Textarea(value='', layout=Layout(height='70px', wid…"
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {},
          "output_type": "display_data"
        }
      ],
      "source": [
        "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
        "from IPython.display import clear_output\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Define the user interface components.\n",
        "question_box = widgets.Textarea(\n",
        "    placeholder=\"Enter your compliance question here.\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\", height=\"70px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "send_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"Send\",\n",
        "    button_style=\"primary\",\n",
        "    tooltip=\"Send the question to Compliance Bot\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "clear_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"Clear chat\",\n",
        "    button_style=\"warning\",\n",
        "    tooltip=\"Clear the conversation history\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "out = widgets.Output()\n",
        "status_label = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Store conversation history as tuples in the format: (role, text, response_data).\n",
        "_history: list = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _render_history():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Render the current conversation history.\"\"\"\n",
        "    for role, text, data in _history:\n",
        "        if role == \"user\":\n",
        "            display(Markdown(f\"---\\n**You:** {text}\"))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            display(Markdown(\"**Compliance Bot:**\"))\n",
        "            _print_result(data)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def on_send(btn):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Submit the current question to the backend and render the response.\"\"\"\n",
        "    question = question_box.value.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not question:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_label.value = \"Waiting for response...\"\n",
        "    send_btn.disabled = True\n",
        "    question_box.value = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    data = _post(\"/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate\", {\"question\": question})\n",
        "\n",
        "    _history.append((\"user\", question, None))\n",
        "    _history.append((\"bot\", \"\", data))\n",
        "\n",
        "    with out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        _render_history()\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_label.value = \"Response received.\"\n",
        "    send_btn.disabled = False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def on_clear(btn):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Clear the conversation history and output area.\"\"\"\n",
        "    _history.clear()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_label.value = \"Chat cleared.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "send_btn.on_click(on_send)\n",
        "clear_btn.on_click(on_clear)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Assemble and display the interface.\n",
        "button_row = widgets.HBox([send_btn, clear_btn, status_label])\n",
        "ui = widgets.VBox([\n",
        "    widgets.HTML(\"<h3>Compliance Bot Chat</h3>\"),\n",
        "    question_box,\n",
        "    button_row,\n",
        "    out\n",
        "])\n",
        "\n",
        "display(ui)"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {},
      "source": [
        "## Cell 8: Run Batch Queries\n",

        "Use this cell to run multiple compliance questions in sequence and compare the results."

        "## Cell 11: Sample Compliance Questions\n",
        "\n",
        "The following examples can be used to validate common query patterns supported by the Compliance Bot.\n",
        "\n",
        "| # | Question | Expected behavior |\n",
        "|---|---|---|\n",
        "| 1 | `How many persons are from the Mumbai circle?` | Executes a count query using the rule-based fast path. |\n",
        "| 2 | `List all senior citizen accounts.` | Executes a select query filtered by `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1`. |\n",
        "| 3 | `Show KYC non-compliant accounts in Delhi.` | Applies multiple filters in the `WHERE` clause. |\n",
        "| 4 | `What is the average balance for Thane branch?` | Executes an aggregate query using `AVG`. |\n",
        "| 5 | `How many accounts are maturing next month?` | Applies a date-range filter on `MTRTY_DT`. |\n",
        "| 6 | `Top 10 accounts by end-of-day balance.` | Executes an ordered query with a row limit. |\n",
        "| 7 | `Count accounts by product name.` | Executes a grouped aggregate query. |\n",
        "| 8 | `Who?` | Triggers the clarification flow. |\n",
        "\n",
        "---\n",
        "\n",
        "## Notes\n",
        "\n",

"""
run_ui.py — Self-contained UI server for Compliance Bot (LangGraph).

Run:
    python run_ui.py

What it does:
  • Embeds the full single-page app as a Python string (no separate index.html needed).
  • Starts Python's built-in HTTP server on port 5000 (override: CB_UI_PORT env var).
  • Proxies every /api/v1/... request to the FastAPI backend
    (default http://0.0.0.0:8066 — override: CB_BACKEND_URL env var).
  • Renders live LangGraph six-node stage traces returned by /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate.
  • Opens the browser automatically.

No extra dependencies beyond the Python standard library.
"""

import json
import os
import sys
import threading
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import webbrowser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

# Configuration
#UI_PORT: int = int(os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT","5001")))
UI_PORT: int = int(os.environ.get('CDSW_APP_PORT'))
BACKEND_URL: str = os.getenv("CB_BACKEND_URL", "http://0.0.0.0:8066").rstrip("/")

      0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
      50%       { opacity: 0.18; }
    }

    .backend-status.alive .led-label { color: #15803d; }
    .backend-status.dead  .led-label { color: #b91c1c; }

    .app.sidebar-collapsed .brand-text,

    .app.sidebar-collapsed .nav-text,

      padding-right: 0;
    }

    }

    }

            <p id="pageSubtitle">Configure the LLM provider used by the LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline.</p>

        <div class="backend-status" id="backendStatus" title="Backend connectivity">

        <section class="page config-page active" data-page="llm-config">

            <div class="stages" id="stageTrail">
              <div class="stage" title="Node 1 (intent.py) — 4 checks: empty, disallowed action word, too short, vague/ambiguous">Intent Checker</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 2 (schema.py) — auto-discovers databases; scores by keyword overlap; builds live schema context">Schema Checker</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 3 (sql_writer_agent.py) — only LLM call; few-shot prompt + live schema; extracts SQL from tags">SQL Writer</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 4 (sql_review.py) — blocks DML/DDL; requires SELECT + FROM; checks table existence; up to 3 retries">SQL Review</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 4.5 (sql_validator.py) — sqlglot AST parse; SELECT-only gate; SQLite dialect canonicalisation">SQL Validator</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 5 (executor.py) — BEGIN DEFERRED; fetchmany(500); always rollback; builds result summary">Executor</div>
            </div>

            <div class="chat-window" id="chatWindow"></div>

            <div class="composer">
              <div class="field">
                <label for="questionInput">Message</label>

                <div class="hint">Press Enter to send. Use Shift + Enter to insert a new line.</div>
              </div>
              <div class="composer-buttons">
                <button class="secondary" id="copyChatBtn">Copy Chat</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="exportChatBtn">Export Chat</button>

  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const provider = document.getElementById('provider');
    const dlhFields = document.getElementById('dlhFields');
    const icaFields = document.getElementById('icaFields');
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');

    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };

    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure the LLM provider — the LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Six LangGraph nodes: Intent Checker → Schema Checker → SQL Writer (LLM) → SQL Review → SQL Validator → Executor.' }
    };

    // Maps a LangGraph stage string prefix to the display name of the stage pill.
    // Prefixes must match the strings emitted by each agent node exactly:
    //   intent.py          → "intent_agent:…"
    //   schema.py          → "schema_agent:…"
    //   sql_writer_agent.py→ "sql_writer_agent:…"
    //   sql_review.py      → "sql_review_agent:…"
    //   sql_validator.py   → "sql_validator_agent:…"
    //   executor.py        → "executor_agent:…"
    const STAGE_PREFIX_MAP = [
      { prefix: 'intent_agent',        label: 'Intent Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'schema_agent',        label: 'Schema Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'sql_writer_agent',    label: 'SQL Writer'    },
      { prefix: 'sql_review_agent',    label: 'SQL Review'    },
      { prefix: 'sql_validator_agent', label: 'SQL Validator' },
      { prefix: 'executor_agent',      label: 'Executor'      },
      { prefix: 'clarify_node',        label: 'Clarification' },
    ];

    /**
     * Highlight the stage pills that appear in the live LangGraph stage trace.
     * Each pill's data-prefix attribute is compared against every stage string
     * returned by the orchestrate endpoint; a match lights the pill up in accent blue.
     */
    function updateStagePills(stages) {
      const trail = document.getElementById('stageTrail');
      if (!trail) return;
      const pills = trail.querySelectorAll('.stage');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.style.background = '';
        pill.style.color = '';
        pill.style.borderColor = '';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '';
      });
      STAGE_PREFIX_MAP.forEach(({ prefix, label }, idx) => {
        const matched = stages.some(s => s.startsWith(prefix));
        if (!matched) return;
        const pill = pills[idx];
        if (!pill) return;
        pill.style.background = '#2563eb';
        pill.style.color = '#ffffff';
        pill.style.borderColor = '#1d4ed8';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '600';
      });
    }

    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }

    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }

    function toggleProviderFields() {
      dlhFields.classList.toggle('hidden', provider.value !== 'dlh');
      icaFields.classList.toggle('hidden', provider.value !== 'ica');
    }

    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }

    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      provider.value = config.provider || 'dlh';
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      document.getElementById('icaBaseUrl').value = config.ica_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('icaApiKey').value = config.ica_api_key || '';
      document.getElementById('icaNamespace').value = config.ica_namespace || 'chat-models';
      toggleProviderFields();
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);

      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }

      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;

      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }

    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      updateStagePills([]); // reset all pills to default (unlit) state
    }

    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }

    async function connectProvider() {
      statusBox.className = 'status';
      statusBox.textContent = '';
      const payload = {
        provider: provider.value,
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value,
        ica_base_url: document.getElementById('icaBaseUrl').value,
        ica_api_key: document.getElementById('icaApiKey').value,
        ica_namespace: document.getElementById('icaNamespace').value
      };

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. LangGraph pipeline is ready.`, 'bot');
        checkBackendHealth();
        setActivePage('chat');
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
      }
    }

    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }

    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    // Render text as markdown (bold + code + pre + newlines).
    function formatNoticeText(text) {
      return text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    // Append a styled notice card (blocked = red, clarify = blue) to the chat.
    function addNotice(text, type) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = `msg-notice ${type}`;
      el.innerHTML = formatNoticeText(text);
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
      chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
    }

    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;

      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
      showTypingIndicator();

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');

        // Update stage pills with live LangGraph trace.
        updateStagePills(data.stages || []);

        const stageTrace = (data.stages || []).join(' → ');
        const stageHtml = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#57606a;margin-bottom:10px;word-break:break-all">${stageTrace || '—'}</div>`;

        // ── Clarification requested (vague or ambiguous question) ──────────
        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.clarification_question, 'clarify');
          return;
        }

        // ── Pipeline error (blocked keyword, max retries, etc.) ────────────
        if (data.error) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.error, 'blocked');
          return;
        }

        // ── Successful query result ────────────────────────────────────────
        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const summary  = data.result_summary || `${rowCount} row(s) returned.`;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        const encodedData = encodeURIComponent(resultsJson);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}` +
          `<strong>Summary</strong><br>${summary.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>')}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>` +
          `<button class="csv-download-btn" data-csv="${encodedData}" onclick="downloadCsv(this)">&#8595; Download as CSV</button>`
        );
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addNotice('An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.', 'blocked');
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }

    function downloadCsv(btn) {
      let rows;
      try { rows = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(btn.dataset.csv)); } catch (_) { return; }
      if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { alert('No data to download.'); return; }
      const headers = Object.keys(rows[0]);
      const escape  = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n')
          ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const csvContent = [
        headers.map(escape).join(','),
        ...rows.map(r => headers.map(h => escape(r[h])).join(','))
      ].join('\r\n');
      const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
      const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a    = document.createElement('a');
      a.href     = url;
      a.download = `results_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/[:T]/g,'-')}.csv`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }

    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
    }

    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });

    provider.addEventListener('change', toggleProviderFields);
    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', loadConfigLocally);
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);

    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
    });

    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });

    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) return;
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
    });

    // ── Backend health polling ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    const backendStatus = document.getElementById('backendStatus');
    const backendLabel  = document.getElementById('backendLabel');

    async function checkBackendHealth() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/health', { method: 'GET', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });
        if (res.ok) {
          backendStatus.className = 'backend-status alive';
          backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Active';
        } else {
          throw new Error('non-ok');
        }
      } catch (_) {
        backendStatus.className = 'backend-status dead';
        backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Offline';
      }
    }

    checkBackendHealth();
    setInterval(checkBackendHealth, 8000);
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
    toggleProviderFields();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Serve the UI and proxy /api/v1/* to the FastAPI backend."""

    # Silence the default per-request access log lines.
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            # Client closed the connection before the response was sent
            # (e.g. browser tab closed, LLM took too long).  Nothing to do.
            pass

    # Serve the HTML page, static assets and proxy GET API calls.
    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]

        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = HTML.encode("utf-8")
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body)
            return

        # Serve static assets (fonts/images) from the assets/ folder
        if path.startswith("/assets/"):
            #assets_root = Path(__file__).parent / "assets"
            assets_root = Path.cwd() / "assets"
            file_path = assets_root / path[len("/assets/"):]
            if file_path.is_file():
                data = file_path.read_bytes()
                ct = "application/octet-stream"
                if file_path.suffix in (".ttf", ".otf"):
                    ct = "font/truetype"
                elif file_path.suffix == ".css":
                    ct = "text/css"
                elif file_path.suffix in (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg"):
                    ct = f"image/{file_path.suffix.lstrip('.')}"
                self._send(200, ct, data)
            else:
                self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        # Proxy GET API calls and the backend health probe
        if path.startswith("/api/") or path == "/health":
            self._proxy_get(path)
            return

        self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _proxy_get(self, path: str) -> None:
        try:
            url = BACKEND_URL + path
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client already disconnected — nothing to send
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)

    # Proxy all POST /api/* requests directly to the FastAPI backend.
    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        raw_body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""

        target_url = BACKEND_URL + path
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            target_url,
            data=raw_body,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
            method="POST",
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client disconnected before response arrived — nothing to send
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            body = exc.read()
            self._send(exc.code, exc.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)


def main() -> None:
    print("CDSW_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT"))
    print("CDSW_READONLY_PORT = ", os.getenv("CDSW_READONLY_PORT"))
    print("CB_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT"))
    print("UI_PORT =", UI_PORT)
    print("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT"))
    
    server = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", UI_PORT), Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}"
    print(f"  Compliance Bot UI  →  {url}")
    print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
    print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")

    # Open browser after a short delay so the server is ready
    threading.Timer(0.8, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")
        sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
