# Compliance Bot v2 — UI Guide

This guide covers every front-end option: the FastAPI HTML UI (`index.html`), the standalone Python server (`run_ui.py`), and the Gradio UI (`gradio_app.py`).

---

## Table of Contents

1. [Overview of UI Options](#overview-of-ui-options)
2. [Option A — FastAPI HTML UI](#option-a--fastapi-html-ui)
3. [Option B — Standalone Python Server (`run_ui.py`)](#option-b--standalone-python-server-run_uipy)
4. [Option C — Gradio UI (`gradio_app.py`)](#option-c--gradio-ui-gradio_apppy)
5. [LLM Configuration Page](#llm-configuration-page)
6. [Chat Page](#chat-page)
7. [Backend Status LED](#backend-status-led)
8. [LangGraph Stage Pills](#langgraph-stage-pills)
9. [Loading Indicator](#loading-indicator)
10. [Sidebar and Navigation](#sidebar-and-navigation)
11. [Keyboard Shortcuts](#keyboard-shortcuts)
12. [Local Storage Persistence](#local-storage-persistence)
13. [Mobile and Responsive Behaviour](#mobile-and-responsive-behaviour)
14. [UI Feature Comparison Table](#ui-feature-comparison-table)

---

## Overview of UI Options

| Option | File | Dependencies | Port | Auto-opens browser |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A — FastAPI HTML | `index.html` served by FastAPI | FastAPI, uvicorn | 8000 | No |
| B — Standalone Python | `run_ui.py` | None (stdlib only) | 5000 | **Yes** |
| C — Gradio | `gradio_app.py` | `gradio>=4.36` | 7860 | **Yes** |

All three UIs connect to the same FastAPI backend at `http://localhost:8000` (configurable).

---

## Option A — FastAPI HTML UI

### Starting

```bash
python main.py
# Then open in your browser: http://localhost:8000
```

The FastAPI lifespan handler:
1. Seeds `COMP_TBL` with 3 sample rows if empty.
2. Builds and compiles the LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline.
3. Initialises the LLM provider from `.env` settings.

FastAPI then serves `index.html` at `GET /` and static fonts under `/assets/`.

---

## Option B — Standalone Python Server (`run_ui.py`)

### Starting

```bash
python run_ui.py
```

The browser opens automatically at `http://localhost:5000`.

### How it works

- Uses Python's built-in `http.server.HTTPServer` — **zero extra dependencies**.
- The full HTML/CSS/JS SPA is embedded as the `HTML` string constant in `run_ui.py`.
- `GET /` returns the embedded HTML.
- `GET /assets/…` is resolved from the local `assets/` folder — Urbanist fonts served correctly.
- `POST /api/v1/…` and `GET /api/v1/…` are **proxied** to the FastAPI backend via `urllib.request`.

### Environment variables

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CDSW_APP_PORT` | `8100` | Port the standalone UI server listens on |
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://0.0.0.0:8066` | FastAPI backend URL for API proxying |

```bash
CB_BACKEND_URL=http://my-server:8000 python run_ui.py
```

---

## Option C — Gradio UI (`gradio_app.py`)

### Starting

```bash
python gradio_app.py
# Opens http://localhost:7860 automatically
```

### Environment variables

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CB_GRADIO_PORT` | `7860` | Gradio server port |
| `CB_BASE_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8000` | FastAPI backend URL |

### Saved configuration

LLM configuration is saved to `.cb_saved_config.json` (equivalent to `localStorage` in the HTML UI).

### Tabs

| Tab | Content |
|---|---|
| ⚙ LLM Configuration | Provider selector, DLH/ICA fields, Test & Save, Load Saved Config, saved-config preview |
| 💬 Chat | LangGraph stage pills, chat bubble window, message textarea, Send / New Chat / Attach / Copy / Export |

---

## LLM Configuration Page

### Fields

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| **Provider** | Dropdown: `DLH (on-prem)` or `ICA` |
| **Model / Resource ID** | Model name or ICA resource ID (default: `google/gemma-4-31b-it`) |
| **DLH Base URL** | DLH inference endpoint (shown when DLH is selected) |
| **DLH API Key** | Bearer token for DLH |
| **ICA Base URL** | ICA API host URL (shown when ICA is selected) |
| **ICA API Key** | ICA developer API key |
| **ICA Namespace** | `chat-models`, `agents`, `assistants`, or `digital-workforce` |

### Actions

| Button | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Test & Save** | Calls `POST /api/v1/config/llm`, tests the connection, **rebuilds the LangGraph pipeline**, saves to localStorage, navigates to Chat on success |
| **Load Saved Config** | Reads the last saved config from localStorage and populates all fields |

### Status indicator

- **Green** — Connection successful. LangGraph pipeline rebuilt with new provider.
- **Red** — Connection failed. The error message from the backend is displayed.

---

## Chat Page

### LangGraph Stage Pills

Five pills represent the five LangGraph agent nodes:

```
[ Intent Agent ]  [ Schema Agent ]  [ SQL Writer ]  [ SQL Review ]  [ Executor ]
```

After each response, pills that correspond to completed agent stages turn **blue**. The mapping:

| Pill | Lights up when stage string starts with |
|---|---|
| Intent Agent | `intent_agent` |
| Schema Agent | `schema_agent` |
| SQL Writer | `sql_writer_agent` |
| SQL Review | `sql_review_agent` |
| Executor | `executor_agent` |

If the Intent Agent requests clarification, a **Clarification** pill appears instead (stage: `clarify_node`).

A compact agent trace line (e.g., `intent_agent:ok → schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db → ...`) is also shown in the bot reply metadata.

### Chat window

- Fixed height of **480 px** with `overflow-y: auto` and `scroll-behavior: smooth`.
- New messages auto-scroll into view.
- **User messages** — right-aligned, blue bubble.
- **Bot messages** — left-aligned, white bubble with border.
- After a successful query the bot reply contains:
  - **Summary** — the `result_summary` from the Executor Agent
  - **SQL** — the generated and reviewed query in a dark code block
  - **Row count** and **full JSON result set** (wraps naturally)
  - **Agent trace** — compact stage string
  - **↓ Download as CSV** — button rendered below the JSON result set; downloads the result rows as a properly-escaped `.csv` file named `results_YYYY-MM-DD-HH-MM-SS.csv`

### Message composer

| Element | Description |
|---|---|
| **Message textarea** | Multi-line input. `Enter` to send, `Shift+Enter` for a new line. |
| **Hint text** | "Press Enter to send. Use Shift + Enter to insert a new line." |

### Composer button layout

```
[ Copy Chat ]  [ Export Chat ]  [ New Chat ]                           [ Send ]
←─────────────── left-aligned ───────────────────────────────────────→ ←right→
```

| Button | Action |
|---|---|
| **Copy Chat** | Copies the plain-text conversation transcript to the clipboard |
| **Export Chat** | Downloads the conversation as `conversation.txt` |
| **New Chat** | Clears the chat window and resets all stage pills |
| **Send** | Submits the question; disabled and shows `Sending…` while the query runs |

### Clarification flow

When the Intent Agent detects a vague question the backend returns `needs_clarification: true` and a `clarification_question`. The bot displays the question and the user types a more specific follow-up.

---

## Backend Status LED

A small pill widget in the **top-right corner of the topbar** shows the live connectivity state of the FastAPI backend.

| State | LED colour | Label |
|---|---|---|
| Checking (startup) | Grey | `Checking…` |
| Backend reachable (`GET /health` returns 2xx) | Solid green | `Backend Active` |
| Backend unreachable or non-2xx | Blinking red | `Backend Offline` |

**Behaviour:**

- The check runs automatically once on page load.
- It repeats every **8 seconds** via `setInterval`.
- When a **Test & Save** succeeds, the health check is triggered immediately so the LED updates without waiting for the next poll interval.
- The UI server proxies `GET /health` to the FastAPI backend — the health endpoint is defined at `GET /health` in `src/main.py`.

---

## LangGraph Stage Pills

See the [Chat Page](#chat-page) section above for pill behaviour. Additional notes:

- Pills reset to grey when **New Chat** is clicked.
- The pill order always matches the LangGraph execution order: Intent → Schema → Writer → Review → Executor.
- If SQL Review rejects the SQL and the Writer retries, the SQL Writer and SQL Review pills both light up (retry cycle).
- In the Gradio UI, pills are implemented via `_build_stage_html()` using `startswith()` prefix matching against the raw stage strings.

---

## Loading Indicator

While a query is in flight:

1. The **Send** button is disabled and changes to `Sending…`.
2. A **three-dot bounce animation** appears at the bottom of the chat window.
3. On response (success or error) the indicator is removed and the Send button is re-enabled.

---

## Sidebar and Navigation

### Collapse / Expand

- Click **◀** to collapse the sidebar to 68 px (icon-only).
- Click **▶** to expand to the saved width.
- State is persisted in `localStorage`.

### Resize

- Drag the vertical **resize handle** on the right edge of the sidebar.
- Minimum width: 220 px. Maximum width: 420 px.
- Width is saved to `localStorage`.

### Navigation items

| Item | Page |
|---|---|
| ⚙ LLM Configuration | Provider setup and connection test |
| 💬 Chat | Conversational NL2SQL query interface |

---

## Keyboard Shortcuts

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| `Enter` (in message box) | Send message |
| `Shift + Enter` (in message box) | Insert a new line |
| `Enter` / `Space` (on nav item) | Navigate to that page |

---

## Local Storage Persistence

The HTML UI and `run_ui.py` persist the following across page refreshes:

| Key | What is stored |
|---|---|
| `cb_sidebar_width` | Sidebar width in pixels |
| `cb_sidebar_collapsed` | `"true"` or `"false"` |
| `cb_active_page` | Last active page ID (`llm-config` or `chat`) |
| `cb_llm_config` | JSON object with all LLM configuration fields |

The Gradio UI saves LLM configuration to `.cb_saved_config.json` on disk.

---

## Mobile and Responsive Behaviour

| Breakpoint | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| ≤ 1100 px | Two-column config page collapses to single column |
| ≤ 920 px | Sidebar becomes an off-canvas drawer; hamburger **☰** appears in the topbar |
| ≤ 640 px | Chat window padding reduces; messages use full width |

On mobile, tap **☰** to open the sidebar. Tap any nav item and the drawer closes automatically.

# Compliance Chatbot v2 — Architecture

## 1. Overview

Compliance Bot v2 is a production-grade NL2SQL banking-compliance system built on **LangGraph**.
A six-node `StateGraph` replaces the old monolithic pipeline. Each node has a single
responsibility; all nodes communicate through a shared `AgentState` TypedDict that flows
through the compiled graph.

**Only one node calls the LLM — `SQLWriterAgent` (`sql_writer_agent.py`).** The remaining
five nodes are deterministic processors (pure-Python, regex, SQLite PRAGMA, or sqlglot AST).

Key components:

| Component | File | Role |
|---|---|---|
| CLI entry-point | `launcher.py` | Starts uvicorn (backend) + `run_ui.py` (UI server) via subprocess |
| FastAPI app | `src/main.py` | Endpoints, lifespan, LangGraph construction |
| Graph factory | `src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py` | Builds and compiles the six-node `StateGraph` |
| Business logic | `src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py` | Thin adapter: `AgentState` ↔ API response models |
| LLM providers | `src/services/model_loader_service.py` | DLH (on-prem) and ICA via OpenAI Python SDK |
| UI server | `run_ui.py` | Stdlib-only HTTP server; proxies `/api/v1/*` and `/health` to backend |

---

## 2. High-Level Architecture

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         Browser  (run_ui.py)                         │
│                                                                       │
│  Topbar    — Backend Status LED (green/red, polls GET /health 8 s)   │
│  Sidebar   — LLM Configuration · Chat navigation                     │
│  Chat page — Stage pills · Chat bubbles · ↓ Download as CSV          │
│  Composer  — Copy Chat · Export Chat · New Chat · Send               │
│  localStorage persistence (sidebar state, LLM config)                │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │  GET /health
                               │  GET /, GET /assets/*
                               │  POST /api/v1/config/llm
                               │  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     FastAPI Application  (src/main.py)               │
│                                                                       │
│  Lifespan  — build_nl2sql_graph()  ·  ModelLoaderService.load()      │
│  GET  /health                — liveness probe                        │
│  POST /api/v1/config/llm     — reconfigure LLM, rebuild graph        │
│  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate  — full agentic pipeline        │
│  POST /api/v1/compliance/chat         — simplified response          │
│  Static /assets/*            — Urbanist fonts                        │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│               Business Logic  (compliance_processor.py)              │
│                                                                       │
│  • Assigns question_id (UUID)                                        │
│  • Builds initial AgentState                                         │
│  • Calls compiled_graph.invoke(state)                                │
│  • Maps final AgentState → ComplianceOrchestrationResponse           │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │  compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              LangGraph StateGraph  (src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py)      │
│                                                                       │
│  [START]                                                              │
│     │                                                                 │
│     ▼                                                                 │
│  intent_agent (intent.py)  ──── intent_ok=False ───► clarify_node    │
│     │                                                      │         │
│     │ intent_ok=True                                       ▼         │
│     ▼                                                   [END]        │
│  schema_agent (schema.py)                                            │
│     │                                                                 │
│     ▼                        ◄────────────────────────────────┐      │
│  sql_writer_agent ◄──────────────────────────────────────┐    │      │
│  (sql_writer_agent.py)                                   │    │      │
│  ★ ONLY NODE THAT CALLS THE LLM ★                        │    │      │
│     │                                                    │    │      │
│     ▼                                                    │    │      │
│  sql_review_agent (sql_review.py)  ── fail,retry ───────┘    │      │
│     │                                                          │      │
│     │ passed                                                   │      │
│     ▼                                                          │      │
│  sql_validator_agent (sql_validator.py)  ── invalid,retry ────┘      │
│     │                                                                 │
│     │ valid                                                           │
│     ▼                                                                 │
│  executor_agent (executor.py)                                        │
│     │                                                                 │
│     ▼                                                                 │
│  [END]                                                                │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     SQLite Databases                                 │
│  data/**/*.db                                                        │
│  • data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db  — default compliance DB             │
│  • additional *.db files are auto-discovered by schema.py            │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 3. Node Responsibilities

Each node in the pipeline has exactly one job. Nodes 1, 2, 4, 4.5, and 5 are
**deterministic processors** — they make no network calls. Only Node 3 calls the LLM.

| Node | File | Class | LLM? | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | `src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py` | `IntentAgent` | ✗ | Keyword-based intent classification; routes to clarification or proceeds |
| **2** | `src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py` | `SchemaAgent` | ✗ | Scans `data/**/*.db`; scores by keyword overlap; builds Markdown schema context |
| **3** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py` | `SQLWriterAgent` | **✓ only one** | Builds few-shot prompt; calls DLH/ICA LLM; extracts SQL from delimiter tags |
| **4** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py` | `SQLReviewAgent` | ✗ | Blocks DML/DDL; requires SELECT + FROM; checks tables exist in live DB |
| **4.5** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py` | `SQLValidatorAgent` | ✗ | sqlglot AST parse; SELECT-type gate; SQLite dialect round-trip canonicalisation |
| **5** | `src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py` | `ExecutorAgent` | ✗ | `BEGIN DEFERRED`; `fetchmany(500)`; always `rollback()`; builds result summary |

---

## 4. Layer Responsibilities

| Layer | Module | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| **Launcher** | [`launcher.py`](../launcher.py) | Starts backend + UI server; watchdog loop; clean shutdown on Ctrl-C |
| **CLI entry-point** | [`main.py`](../main.py) | Parse args; launch uvicorn with `src.main:app` |
| **Config** | [`config/settings.py`](../config/settings.py) | Centralised settings via Pydantic `BaseSettings` |
| **API** | [`src/main.py`](../src/main.py) | HTTP routing, lifespan, LangGraph construction, static-file serving |
| **Business Logic** | [`src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py`](../src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py) | Invokes graph; maps `AgentState` ↔ Pydantic response models |
| **Graph** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py) | Assembles `StateGraph`; node registration; conditional edge routing |
| **State** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/state.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/state.py) | Shared `AgentState` TypedDict (`total=False`); `stages` uses `operator.add` reducer |
| **Node 1** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py) | Rule-based intent classification; clarification detection — **no LLM** |
| **Node 2** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py) | Live SQLite schema introspection; DB selection by keyword score — **no LLM** |
| **Node 3** ★ | [`src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py) | **LLM SQL generation** — the only node that calls the LLM |
| **Node 4** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py) | DML/DDL blocking; `SELECT` + `FROM` checks; table-existence validation; retry loop — **no LLM** |
| **Node 4.5** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py) | sqlglot AST parse; statement-type check; SQLite dialect canonicalisation — **no LLM** |
| **Node 5** | [`src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py) | Read-only SQLite execution; result summary — **no LLM** |
| **LLM** | [`src/services/model_loader_service.py`](../src/services/model_loader_service.py) | DLH / ICA provider lifecycle and inference |
| **Models** | [`src/models/schemas.py`](../src/models/schemas.py) | Pydantic request / response shapes |
| **Schema metadata** | [`src/models/compliance_schema.py`](../src/models/compliance_schema.py) | Column descriptions + few-shot LLM prompts |
| **UI server** | [`run_ui.py`](../run_ui.py) | Stdlib-only server; backend status LED; CSV download; proxies `/api/v1/*` + `/health` |
| **Exceptions** | [`src/exceptions/custom_exceptions.py`](../src/exceptions/custom_exceptions.py) | `ComplianceBotError` hierarchy; safe client-facing messages |
| **Utils** | [`src/utils/logger.py`](../src/utils/logger.py) | Centralised logging factory |
| **Utils** | [`src/utils/constants.py`](../src/utils/constants.py) | Shared constants (`SQL_BEGIN_TAG`, `SQL_END_TAG`, table names) |

---

## 5. Runtime Request / Response Flow

```
Browser (run_ui.py)
  │
  │  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate
  │  {"question": "How many senior citizens in the Thane branch?"}
  │
  ▼
FastAPI  src/main.py
  │  validates payload → ComplianceChatRequest
  │
  ▼
ComplianceProcessor.process_query_with_orchestration()
  │  builds AgentState = {question, question_id, stages: []}
  │
  ▼
compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
  │
  ├─► intent.py :: IntentAgent.run(state)              [no LLM]
  │       • normalises question (strip, add "?")
  │       • checks _METRIC_TERMS vs _VAGUE_PHRASES / _AMBIGUOUS_TOKENS
  │       • intent_ok=True → continue  |  False → ClarifyNode → [END]
  │
  ├─► schema.py :: SchemaAgent.run(state)              [no LLM]
  │       • glob data/**/*.db
  │       • score each DB by keyword overlap with question
  │       • PRAGMA table_info → Markdown schema context
  │       • writes db_path + schema_context
  │
  ├─► sql_writer_agent.py :: SQLWriterAgent.run(state) [★ LLM call]
  │       • builds [INST]…[/INST] few-shot prompt
  │       • ModelLoaderService.generate(prompt) → raw LLM output
  │       • extract_sql() strips <begin_sql_response>…<end_sql_response>
  │       • writes sql_query
  │
  ├─► sql_review.py :: SQLReviewAgent.run(state)       [no LLM]
  │       • rejects DML/DDL keywords (regex, whole-word)
  │       • requires query to start with SELECT
  │       • requires FROM clause
  │       • checks every table referenced exists in db_path
  │       • PASS → sql_validator_agent
  │       • FAIL → sql_writer_agent (retry, max 3×, with feedback)
  │
  ├─► sql_validator.py :: SQLValidatorAgent.run(state) [no LLM]
  │       • sqlglot.parse(sql, dialect="sqlite") — full AST parse
  │       • assert statement type == Select
  │       • round-trip to SQLite dialect — canonicalise SQL
  │       • VALID  → writes canonical sql_query → executor_agent
  │       • INVALID → writes review_feedback → sql_writer_agent (retry)
  │
  └─► executor.py :: ExecutorAgent.run(state)          [no LLM]
          • sqlite3.connect(db_path)
          • conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")
          • cursor.execute(sql_query)  →  fetchmany(500)
          • conn.rollback() + conn.close()  (always, even on success)
          • _build_summary() → result_summary
          • writes result_rows, result_columns, result_summary
  │
  ▼
ComplianceProcessor._map_state_to_response()
  │  AgentState → ComplianceOrchestrationResponse
  │
  ▼
Browser  ←  200 OK + sql_query + df_output + result_summary + stages[]
```

---

## 6. Startup Flow

```
python launcher.py
  │
  ├─► subprocess: uvicorn src.main:app  (backend, port CB_BACKEND_PORT=8001)
  │       │
  │       └─► lifespan: _build_processor(model_settings)
  │                  ModelLoaderService.load()    ← connects to DLH or ICA
  │                  build_nl2sql_graph(model_service, db_path)
  │                      └─► StateGraph.compile()
  │                  ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph)
  │
  ├─► (wait BACKEND_WARMUP_SECONDS=7 for backend to initialise)
  │
  └─► subprocess: python run_ui.py  (UI server, port CDSW_APP_PORT=8100)

# Before first run — seed the compliance database:
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
```

---

## 7. LangGraph State Design

All data flows through a single `AgentState` TypedDict in
[`src/nl2sql_agents/state.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/state.py).
Each node reads only the keys it needs and writes back only the keys it owns.
LangGraph merges partial dicts automatically; the `stages` list uses an
`operator.add` reducer so every node appends rather than overwrites.

| Field | Written by | Read by |
|---|---|---|
| `question` | Caller | `intent.py` |
| `interpreted_question` | `intent.py` | `schema.py`, `sql_writer_agent.py` |
| `intent_ok` | `intent.py` | Graph router |
| `clarification_needed` | `intent.py` | `ComplianceProcessor` |
| `schema_context` | `schema.py` | `sql_writer_agent.py` |
| `db_path` | `schema.py` | `sql_review.py`, `executor.py` |
| `sql_query` | `sql_writer_agent.py`; `sql_validator.py` (canonical) | `sql_review.py`, `executor.py` |
| `sql_review_passed` | `sql_review.py`; `sql_validator.py` (on failure) | Graph router |
| `review_feedback` | `sql_review.py`; `sql_validator.py` (on failure) | `sql_writer_agent.py` (retry) |
| `retry_count` | `sql_review.py`; `sql_validator.py` (on failure) | `sql_review.py` (ceiling check) |
| `sqlglot_valid` | `sql_validator.py` | Graph router |
| `sqlglot_error` | `sql_validator.py` | `ComplianceProcessor`, UI |
| `result_rows` | `executor.py` | `ComplianceProcessor` |
| `result_columns` | `executor.py` | `ComplianceProcessor`, UI |
| `result_summary` | `executor.py` | `ComplianceProcessor`, UI |
| `stages` | Every node (appended) | `ComplianceProcessor`, UI stage pills |
| `error` | Any node | `ComplianceProcessor` |

---

## 8. Security Design

| Concern | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| SQL injection | `sql_review.py` blocks DML/DDL keywords before execution; `sql_validator.py` adds an independent AST-level gate |
| Unsafe mutation | `sql_review.py` rejects non-SELECT; `executor.py` wraps every query in `BEGIN DEFERRED` + unconditional `rollback()` |
| Malformed SQL | `sql_validator.py` uses sqlglot AST parse — structurally broken queries fail before reaching `executor.py` |
| Non-SELECT at AST level | `sql_validator.py` checks `type(stmt).__name__` in the parsed AST, independent of regex |
| Secret leakage | Provider config loaded from `.env` (git-ignored); no hard-coded credentials |
| Wrong DB selection | Keyword-scored selector in `schema.py` with default fallback |
| Stack-trace exposure | `ComplianceBotError` subclasses expose only `message`, never internal `detail`, to API clients |
| Retry abuse | `sql_review.py` enforces `max_retries=3`; hard-stops with error state after ceiling is reached |

---

## 9. Technology Stack

| Component | Technology |
|---|---|
| API framework | FastAPI 0.111+ |
| Agentic pipeline | LangGraph 0.2+ |
| Data validation | Pydantic v2 |
| LLM providers | DLH (on-prem) and ICA (IBM Consulting Advantage) via OpenAI Python SDK |
| Database | SQLite 3 (bundled with Python stdlib) |
| SQL validation | sqlglot ≥ 25.0.0 — pure-Python AST parser and transpiler |
| Configuration | pydantic-settings + `.env` |
| Logging | Python `logging` module |
| UI server | Python `http.server.HTTPServer` (stdlib only, zero extra deps) |
| UI font | Urbanist (18 weights, self-hosted) |
| Testing | pytest + httpx |
| Python | ≥ 3.11 |

####################################################################################################

# Feature Guide — Compliance Bot v2 (LangGraph)

A detailed walkthrough of every feature.

---

## 1. Provider Configuration

The application supports two remote LLM providers configured through `POST /api/v1/config/llm` or through the UI **Test & Save** button. Saving a new configuration **rebuilds the entire LangGraph pipeline** using the new `ModelLoaderService` instance.

### DLH (on-prem)

Uses the OpenAI Python SDK with a custom `base_url` pointing to the DLH inference server. Always sends `temperature` and `max_tokens`. TLS verification is disabled by default (self-signed certs on internal networks).

### ICA (IBM Consulting Advantage)

Uses the OpenAI Python SDK with a namespace-specific `base_url`:

```
{ica_base_url}/ica/v1/{namespace}/
```

Sets `Authorization: Bearer <key>` as a default header. Does **not** send `temperature`/`max_tokens` by default (enable with `MODEL__ICA_SEND_TEMPERATURE=true`).

Supported namespaces: `chat-models`, `agents`, `assistants`, `digital-workforce`.

---

## 2. LangGraph Agentic Pipeline

Every request to `POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate` passes through a compiled LangGraph `StateGraph` with six nodes. The pipeline is defined in [`src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py`](../src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py).

```
[START]
   │
   ▼
Node 1 — IntentAgent  (intent.py)
   • Normalises the question
   • ★ Check 1: disallowed action words (DELETE, DROP, INSERT, UPDATE …)
   │     └─► If found → intent_ok=False → ClarifyNode [END]
   │             UI shows a RED blocked card with the offending keyword
   • ★ Check 2: vague/ambiguous phrasing ("show", "details", "show me this" …)
   │     └─► If matched → intent_ok=False → ClarifyNode [END]
   │             UI shows a BLUE clarification card with example questions
   • If both pass → intent_ok=True
   │
   ▼  [intent_ok=True]
Node 2 — SchemaAgent  (schema.py)
   • Scans data/**/*.db for available databases
   • Scores each by keyword overlap with the question
   • Reads actual SQLite PRAGMA table_info
   • Builds Markdown schema context for the LLM prompt
   │
   ▼
Node 3 — SQLWriterAgent  (sql_writer_agent.py)  ★ ONLY LLM CALL
   • Calls the LLM with a structured few-shot prompt
   • Injects live schema context + few-shot examples
   • Extracts SQL from <begin_sql_response>…<end_sql_response> tags
   │
   ▼
Node 4 — SQLReviewAgent  (sql_review.py)
   • ★ Checks LLM-generated SQL for disallowed keywords
   │     └─► If found → sets error (user-facing RED card) + retries LLM
   • Requires query to start with SELECT
   • Requires FROM clause
   • Checks every referenced table exists in the selected database
   │
   ├─► [review failed, retry_count < 3] ──► SQLWriterAgent (with feedback)
   │
   ▼  [review passed]
Node 4.5 — SQLValidatorAgent  (sql_validator.py)
   • sqlglot AST parse — catches structurally broken SQL
   • Statement-type gate — SELECT only at AST level
   • SQLite dialect round-trip canonicalisation
   │
   ▼  [valid]
Node 5 — ExecutorAgent  (executor.py)
   • Opens SQLite connection with BEGIN DEFERRED (read-only)
   • Executes the SQL; fetchmany(500)
   • Always rolls back and closes connection
   • Builds result_summary string
   │
   ▼
[END]
```

The `stages` list in the response carries the raw trace of every step that ran.

---

## 3. Read-Only SQL Enforcement — Three Layers

### Layer 0 — IntentAgent (before LLM call)

The very first check — happens **before** the schema is read or the LLM is called.

`intent.py` checks the user's raw question for disallowed action words:

```
delete  drop  insert  update  truncate  alter  create  replace
pragma  attach  detach  vacuum  modify  remove  erase  wipe  purge  destroy
```

If any of these words appear as whole tokens, the pipeline terminates immediately with `intent_ok=False`. The UI shows a **red blocked card** naming the offending keyword and directing the user to ask a read-only question.

### Layer 1 — SQLReviewAgent (LLM-generated SQL)

Even if the user's question passes Layer 0, the LLM might still produce SQL containing a disallowed keyword. `sql_review.py` catches this with a whole-word regex check on the generated SQL:

```
INSERT  UPDATE  DELETE  DROP  TRUNCATE  ALTER  CREATE  REPLACE  PRAGMA  ATTACH  DETACH  VACUUM
```

If found, the agent sets `sql_review_passed=False`, sets a user-facing `error` (shown as a **red blocked card** in the UI), increments `retry_count`, and routes back to `SQLWriterAgent` with corrective feedback. After 3 failed retries the pipeline surfaces the error to the user.

### Layer 2 — ExecutorAgent (at execution)

Even if everything above passes, every database connection is wrapped in:

```python
conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")
cursor.execute(sql_query)
# ... fetchmany ...
conn.rollback()   # always — even on success
conn.close()
```

`BEGIN DEFERRED` prevents any accidental write from taking effect; `rollback()` in the `finally` block ensures this even if an exception is raised mid-execution.

---

## 4. Clarification Flow

When the Intent Agent detects a vague or ambiguous question (e.g., `"show"`, `"details"`, `"show me this"`):

1. Sets `intent_ok = False`
2. Sets `clarification_needed` to a friendly prompt with example questions
3. Graph routes to `ClarifyNode` (terminal) — no schema read, no SQL, no execution
4. Response returns `needs_clarification: true` + `clarification_question`

The UI displays the clarification message as a **blue clarification card** with formatted bullet-point examples. The user types a more specific follow-up and sends again.

**What triggers clarification:**
- Bare vague phrases: `"show"`, `"details"`, `"info"`, `"data"`, `"give me"`, `"tell me"`
- Ambiguous pronouns without a domain term: `"show me this"`, `"what about that"`

---

## 6. Multi-Database Routing (via SchemaAgent)

The `SchemaAgent` handles database discovery and selection on every request — no startup-time cache needed.

**How it works:**
1. Scans `data/**/*.db` for all SQLite files
2. For each file, connects and reads table/column names via `PRAGMA table_info`
3. Scores each database: +1 per matching column name in the question, +2 per matching table name
4. Selects the highest-scoring database; falls back to the configured default if no match

Adding a new database is as simple as placing a `.db` file under `data/` — no restart needed beyond the next query.

---

## 7. SQLite Database Setup

The database is **not** seeded automatically. Before first use, run:

```bash
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
```

This creates `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db`, creates `COMP_TBL` with the full 25-column schema, and inserts 2,000 realistic synthetic rows. See [`docs/synthetic-data.md`](synthetic-data.md) for full details.

---

## 8. UI Features

### Blocked keyword card

When a disallowed keyword is detected (in the user's question or in the LLM-generated SQL), the UI renders a **red notice card** instead of a normal bot message:

- Clearly names the blocked keyword in bold
- States this is a read-only interface
- Provides three example valid questions
- No stage pills are lit for queries blocked at the intent layer

### Clarification card

When the question is too vague or ambiguous, the UI renders a **blue clarification card**:

- Explains the question needs more detail
- Shows four formatted example questions as bullet points
- Asks the user to rephrase

### Live LangGraph stage trail

After each response, the five agent node pills update visually:

```
[ Intent Agent ]  [ Schema Agent ]  [ SQL Writer ]  [ SQL Review ]  [ Executor ]
```

Pills turn blue as the corresponding agent runs. The raw stage trace (e.g., `intent_agent:ok → schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db → ...`) is also shown as a compact text line in the bot reply.

### Result summary

The `ExecutorAgent` produces a `result_summary` string for the response:

- Scalar aggregation (`COUNT`, `SUM`): `Result: **record_count** = \`1\``
- Multi-row query: `Query returned **N record(s)**. Columns: COL1, COL2, ...`
- Empty result: `The query executed successfully but returned no matching records.`

### Loading animation

A three-dot bounce animation appears as soon as a message is sent and disappears when the response arrives. The Send button is disabled and shows `Sending…` during this time.

### Scrollable chat

Fixed-height (480 px) scrollable container with `scroll-behavior: smooth`. New messages auto-scroll into view.

### Full result rendering

SQL results are rendered as a full JSON code block — no truncation. Long values wrap naturally using `white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-all`.

### Local storage persistence

| Key | Stored value |
|---|---|
| `cb_sidebar_width` | Sidebar width in pixels |
| `cb_sidebar_collapsed` | Collapsed state |
| `cb_active_page` | Last active page |
| `cb_llm_config` | All LLM configuration fields |

---

## 9. Logging

Rotating log files are written to `logs/compliance_bot.log` using size-based rotation (`RotatingFileHandler` — 10 MB per file, 10 backup files). Size-based rotation is used instead of time-based rotation because Windows does not allow renaming an open file, which `TimedRotatingFileHandler` requires at midnight.

```
2026-07-29 11:05:54 | INFO  | compliance_bot.nl2sql_agents.intent_agent | IntentAgent — intent resolved: 'How many ...?'
2026-07-29 11:05:54 | INFO  | compliance_bot.nl2sql_agents.schema_agent | SchemaAgent — selected db=COMP_REPORTS.db
2026-07-29 11:05:54 | INFO  | compliance_bot.nl2sql_agents.sql_writer_agent | SQLWriterAgent — rule-based SQL: SELECT COUNT(*)...
```

Log level is controlled by `LOG_LEVEL` in `.env` (default: `INFO`). Set to `DEBUG` for verbose output.

## The Six-Node Agent Pipeline

```
User question
     │
     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 1 — IntentAgent  (intent.py)                              │
│  Rule-based. Check 1: empty. Check 2: disallowed action words.  │
│  Check 3: short/meaningless input. Check 4: vague/ambiguous     │
│  (skipped when question has >3 meaningful tokens).              │
│  intent_ok=True → proceed  │  intent_ok=False → clarify + END  │
└────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 2 — SchemaAgent                                           │
│  Scans data/**/*.db. Scores databases by keyword overlap.       │
│  Reads PRAGMA table_info. Builds Markdown schema + sample rows. │
└────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 3 — SQLWriterAgent  ★ ONLY LLM CALL                      │
│  Pure LLM path — no rule-based fast path.                       │
│  Few-shot prompt + live schema context injected.                │
│  Extracts SQL from <begin_sql_response>…<end_sql_response> tags.│
└────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 4 — SQLReviewAgent                                        │
│  Blocks DML/DDL keywords. Requires SELECT + FROM.              │
│  Checks table existence. Retries writer up to 3× with feedback.│
└────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 4.5 — SQLValidatorAgent  ← NEW                           │
│  sqlglot AST parse check. Statement-type gate (SELECT only).   │
│  SQLite dialect round-trip canonicalisation.                    │
└────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Node 5 — ExecutorAgent                                         │
│  BEGIN DEFERRED transaction. fetchmany(500). Rollback.         │
│  Returns result_rows, result_columns, result_summary.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
     │
     ▼
ComplianceOrchestrationResponse (all stages, SQL, rows, summary)
```

---

## Technology Stack

| Component | Technology | Version |
|---|---|---|
| API framework | FastAPI | ≥ 0.111 |
| Agentic pipeline | LangGraph | ≥ 0.2 |
| Data validation | Pydantic v2 | ≥ 2.7 |
| LLM providers | OpenAI Python SDK | ≥ 1.35 |
| Database | SQLite 3 | Bundled with Python |
| SQL AST validation | sqlglot | ≥ 25.0.0 |
| Configuration | pydantic-settings + `.env` | ≥ 2.3 |
| Logging | Python `logging` + YAML | — |
| Gradio UI | Gradio | ≥ 4.36 |
| Streamlit UI | Streamlit + pandas | ≥ 1.35 / ≥ 2.0 |
| Testing | pytest + httpx | ≥ 8.2 / ≥ 0.27 |
| Python | CPython | ≥ 3.11 |

     SECTION 2 — HOW TO USE
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->

# Part 2 · How to Use

A complete step-by-step guide — from cloning the repo to asking your first compliance question.

## Prerequisites

| Requirement | Minimum version | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Python | 3.11 | 3.12 / 3.14 also tested |
| pip | 23+ | Included with Python |
| An LLM endpoint | — | DLH (on-prem) **or** IBM ICA |
| SQLite | 3.x | Bundled with Python — no install needed |

No Docker, no Redis, no Node.js required.

---

## Installation

```bash
# 1. Clone the repository
git clone <repo-url>
cd COMPLIANCE-BOT-V2-LANGRAPH

# 2. Create and activate a virtual environment (recommended)
python -m venv .venv

# Windows
.venv\Scripts\activate

# Linux / macOS
source .venv/bin/activate

# 3. Install dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

---

## Configuration

### Step 1 — Create your `.env` file

```bash
# Windows
copy .env.example .env
notepad .env

# Linux / macOS
cp .env.example .env
nano .env
```

### Step 2 — Choose your LLM provider

#### DLH (on-premises OpenAI-compatible endpoint)

```env
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://<your-dlh-host>:<port>/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL=false
```

#### ICA (IBM Consulting Advantage)

```env
MODEL__PROVIDER=ica
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=your-resource-id
MODEL__ICA_BASE_URL=https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com
MODEL__ICA_API_KEY=your-developer-key
MODEL__ICA_NAMESPACE=chat-models
MODEL__ICA_SEND_TEMPERATURE=false
```

---

## Database Setup

The database is **not** populated automatically. Run the seeder before starting the app for the first time:

```bash
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
```

```bash
# Re-run on an existing database — no harm done
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 500    # append 500 more rows

# Wipe and start fresh
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000

# Create DB + table only, no rows (useful for CI)
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 0
```

---

## Starting the Application

### Option A — FastAPI HTML UI (default)

```bash
python main.py
```

Open **http://localhost:8000** (or whatever `PORT` is set to in `.env`).

```bash
python main.py --port 9000
python main.py --host 127.0.0.1 --port 9000
python main.py --reload            # hot-reload for development
```

### Option B — Standalone Python UI

```bash
# Terminal 1 — start the FastAPI backend
python main.py

# Terminal 2 — start the standalone UI
python run_ui.py
```

Open **http://localhost:5000**.

```bash
# Windows PowerShell
$env:CB_UI_PORT = "6000"
$env:CB_BACKEND_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8080"
python run_ui.py
```

### Option C — Gradio UI

```bash
# Terminal 1 — backend
python main.py

# Terminal 2 — Gradio
python gradio_app.py
```

Open **http://localhost:7860**.

---

## Asking Questions

| Category | Example questions |
|---|---|
| **Counts** | `How many senior citizens are there?` |
| | `How many accounts have KYC flag N?` |
| **Filters** | `Show accounts where age is more than 60 and KYC is N` |
| | `List all accounts with balance greater than 500000` |
| **Aggregates** | `What is the average balance of senior citizens?` |
| | `Show total balance by circle` |
| **Lookups** | `Show all accounts for branch VIKRAM SARABHAI BHAWAN` |

### What the six agent stages mean

| Stage badge | File | What it did |
|---|---|---|
| **Intent Agent** | `intent.py` | Normalised question; ran 4 checks (empty → disallowed word → too short → vague); routed to clarify if failed |
| **Schema Agent** | `schema.py` | Scanned `data/**/*.db`, selected the best database by keyword overlap |
| **SQL Writer** | `sql_writer_agent.py` | Called the LLM with few-shot prompt + live schema context; extracted SQL |
| **SQL Review** | `sql_review.py` | Checked LLM SQL for DML/DDL keywords, SELECT, FROM, table existence; up to 3 retries |
| **SQL Validator** | `sql_validator.py` | sqlglot AST parse; SELECT-only gate; SQLite dialect canonicalisation |
| **Executor** | `executor.py` | Ran the query read-only (`BEGIN DEFERRED`/rollback), formatted result rows |

---

## Using the API directly

```bash
curl http://localhost:8000/health
```

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "How many senior citizens have KYC flag N?"}'
```

```bash
# Switch provider at runtime — no restart required
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"provider":"ica","model_name":"your-id","ica_api_key":"your-key","ica_namespace":"chat-models"}'
```

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `[Errno 10048] address already in use` | Port occupied | Kill the process or change `PORT` in `.env` |
| `HTTP 503 — Compliance processor is not initialised` | LLM connection failed | Use `POST /api/v1/config/llm` or check `.env` |
| `no such table: COMP_TBL` | Database not seeded | Run `python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000` |
| `ModelLoadError: ICA API key is required` | `MODEL__ICA_API_KEY` is empty | Add your ICA key to `.env` |
| `SqlGenerationError: SQL delimiter tags not found` | LLM did not wrap SQL in expected tags | Lower `MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE` |
| Clarification badge fires for every question | Question too vague | Add a specific filter |
| `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'langgraph'` | Dependencies not installed | Run `pip install -r requirements.txt` |

---

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 3 — ARCHITECTURE
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->

# Part 3 · Architecture

## Overview

Compliance Bot v2 is a production-grade NL2SQL banking-compliance system built on **LangGraph**. A six-node `StateGraph` replaces the old monolithic service pipeline. Each agent node has a single responsibility; they communicate through a shared `AgentState` TypedDict.

---

## High-Level Architecture

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         Browser UI / Client                          │
│  index.html  /  run_ui.py  /  gradio_app.py                          │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │ POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     FastAPI Application Layer                        │
│  src/main.py  ←  started by  main.py  (root CLI entry-point)         │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     Business Logic Layer                             │
│  src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py                          │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │  compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  LangGraph StateGraph (6 agent nodes)                │
│                                                                      │
│  [START] → IntentAgent → SchemaAgent → SQLWriterAgent                │
│         → SQLReviewAgent → SQLValidatorAgent → ExecutorAgent → [END] │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     SQLite Databases                                 │
│  data/**/*.db  (auto-created; git-ignored)                           │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Layer Responsibilities

| Layer | Module | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| **CLI entry-point** | `main.py` | Parse args; launch uvicorn with `src.main:app` |
| **Config** | `config/settings.py` | Centralised settings via Pydantic BaseSettings |
| **API** | `src/main.py` | HTTP routing, lifespan, LangGraph construction, UI serving |
| **Business Logic** | `src/business_logic/compliance_processor.py` | Invokes graph; maps `AgentState` ↔ Pydantic response |
| **Graph** | `src/nl2sql_agents/graph.py` | Assembles `StateGraph`; conditional edge routing |
| **Node 1** | `src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py` | Rule-based: 4-check intent classification (empty / disallowed word / too short / vague); skip ambiguous check when >3 meaningful tokens |
| **Node 2** | `src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py` | Live SQLite schema introspection; DB selection by keyword scoring |
| **Node 3** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py` | **Only LLM call** — pure LLM SQL generation; few-shot + live schema prompt |
| **Node 4** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py` | Safety, SELECT, FROM, table-existence; user-facing error on blocked keyword; retry loop (max 3) |
| **Node 4.5** | `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py` | sqlglot AST parse, statement-type check, SQLite dialect canonicalisation |
| **Node 5** | `src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py` | Read-only SQLite execution (`BEGIN DEFERRED`/rollback); result summary |
| **LLM** | `src/services/model_loader_service.py` | DLH / ICA provider lifecycle and inference |

---

## Runtime Request / Response Flow

```
Client
  │  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate
  │  {"question": "How many senior citizens in the Thane branch?"}
  ▼
FastAPI  src/main.py  → validates → ComplianceChatRequest
  ▼
ComplianceProcessor.process_query_with_orchestration()
  ▼
compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
  ├─► IntentAgent     — normalise / check intent
  ├─► SchemaAgent     — scan DBs, score, build schema context
  ├─► SQLWriterAgent  — rule-based or LLM SQL
  ├─► SQLReviewAgent  — safety + schema checks (max 3 retries)
  ├─► SQLValidatorAgent — sqlglot AST parse + canonicalise
  └─► ExecutorAgent   — BEGIN DEFERRED; fetchmany(500); rollback
  ▼
ComplianceProcessor._map_state_to_response()
  ▼
Client  ← 200 OK + full result set + stage trace
```

---

## LangGraph State Design

| Field | Written by | Read by |
|---|---|---|
| `question` | Caller | IntentAgent |
| `interpreted_question` | IntentAgent | SchemaAgent, SQLWriterAgent |
| `intent_ok` | IntentAgent | Graph router |
| `schema_context` | SchemaAgent | SQLWriterAgent |
| `db_path` | SchemaAgent | SQLReviewAgent, ExecutorAgent |
| `sql_query` | SQLWriterAgent; SQLValidatorAgent (normalised) | SQLReviewAgent, ExecutorAgent |
| `sql_review_passed` | SQLReviewAgent; SQLValidatorAgent | Graph router |
| `review_feedback` | SQLReviewAgent; SQLValidatorAgent | SQLWriterAgent (retry) |
| `retry_count` | SQLReviewAgent; SQLValidatorAgent | SQLReviewAgent (ceiling) |
| `sqlglot_valid` | SQLValidatorAgent | Graph router |
| `sqlglot_error` | SQLValidatorAgent | ComplianceProcessor, UI |
| `result_rows` | ExecutorAgent | ComplianceProcessor |
| `result_summary` | ExecutorAgent | ComplianceProcessor, UI |
| `stages` | Every node (appended) | ComplianceProcessor, UI |
| `error` | Any node | ComplianceProcessor |

---

## Security Design

| Concern | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| SQL injection | LIKE values escaped in rule-based fast path |
| Unsafe mutation | `SQLReviewAgent` blocks DML/DDL; `ExecutorAgent` uses `BEGIN DEFERRED` + rollback |
| Malformed SQL | `SQLValidatorAgent` uses sqlglot AST parse |
| Non-SELECT at AST level | `SQLValidatorAgent` checks statement type in parsed AST |
| Secret leakage | Provider config loaded from `.env` (git-ignored) |
| Stack-trace exposure | `ComplianceBotError` exposes only `message`, never `detail` |
| Retry abuse | `SQLReviewAgent` enforces `max_retries=3` |

---

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 4 — API REFERENCE
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->

# Part 4 · API Reference

All endpoints are served by `src/main.py`. Base URL: `http://localhost:8000`.

Interactive docs: **Swagger UI** → `http://localhost:8000/docs` · **ReDoc** → `http://localhost:8000/redoc`

---

## Endpoints

### GET /

Serves the HTML UI (`index.html`). Returns `text/html`.

### GET /health

```json
{ "status": "ok", "version": "2.0.0" }
```

### POST /api/v1/config/llm

Configure and test an LLM provider. On success, **rebuilds the LangGraph pipeline**.

**curl — DLH:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"provider":"dlh","model_name":"google/gemma-4-31b-it","dlh_base_url":"http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1","dlh_api_key":"EMPTY","dlh_verify_ssl":false}'
```

**curl — ICA:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"provider":"ica","model_name":"your-resource-id","ica_base_url":"https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com","ica_api_key":"your-api-key","ica_namespace":"chat-models","ica_send_temperature":false}'
```

### POST /api/v1/compliance/chat

Simplified NL2SQL. Returns `sql_query`, `df_output`, `error`.

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "Show all senior citizen accounts."}'
```

### POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate

Full pipeline. Returns complete stage trace, SQL, results, and summary.

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?"}'
```

**Success response:**
```json
{
  "question_id": "7f3c9d1a-...",
  "interpreted_question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?",
  "needs_clarification": false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE UPPER(CIRCLENAME) LIKE '%MUMBAI%';",
  "df_output": [{"record_count": 1}],
  "result_summary": "Result: **record_count** = `1`",
  "stages": ["intent_agent:ok","schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db","sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0","sql_review_agent:passed","executor_agent:ok rows=1"],
  "error": null
}
```

---

## Request Schemas

### ComplianceChatRequest

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `question` | `string \| null` | No | Natural-language compliance question |
| `question_id` | `string \| null` | No | Optional idempotency key; auto-assigned UUID if omitted |

### LLMConnectionRequest

| Field | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `provider` | `string` | **Yes** | — | `"dlh"` or `"ica"` |
| `model_name` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | Model name or ICA resource ID |
| `dlh_base_url` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | DLH inference base URL |
| `dlh_api_key` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | DLH bearer token |
| `dlh_verify_ssl` | `boolean` | No | `false` | Verify DLH TLS certificate |
| `ica_base_url` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | ICA host URL |
| `ica_api_key` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | ICA developer API key |
| `ica_namespace` | `string \| null` | No | From settings | ICA namespace |
| `ica_send_temperature` | `boolean` | No | `false` | Forward temperature/max_tokens to ICA |

---

## Response Schemas

### ComplianceOrchestrationResponse

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `question_id` | `string \| null` | Echo of the request `question_id` |
| `interpreted_question` | `string \| null` | Normalised question |
| `needs_clarification` | `boolean` | `true` when Intent Agent requests more detail |
| `clarification_question` | `string \| null` | Follow-up question text |
| `sql_query` | `string \| null` | Generated and reviewed SQL |
| `df_output` | `array \| null` | Result rows as `{column: value}` dicts |
| `result_summary` | `string \| null` | Human-readable summary from Executor Agent |
| `stages` | `array[string]` | Ordered LangGraph node stage strings |
| `error` | `string \| null` | Pipeline-level error |

### Stage String Reference

| Stage string pattern | Agent node | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `intent_agent:ok` | IntentAgent | Question is clear; pipeline proceeds |
| `intent_agent:empty` | IntentAgent | Empty question received |
| `intent_agent:clarification_required` | IntentAgent | Vague/ambiguous question; clarification requested |
| `intent_agent:too_short` | IntentAgent | Input too short or meaningless |
| `intent_agent:disallowed_action_word=<KW>` | IntentAgent | Disallowed keyword in raw question; pipeline halted |
| `schema_agent:db=<filename>` | SchemaAgent | Schema read from named DB file |
| `sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=N` | SQLWriterAgent | SQL generated by LLM on attempt N (0 = first) |
| `sql_writer_agent:extraction_failed` | SQLWriterAgent | LLM response missing SQL delimiter tags |
| `sql_review_agent:passed` | SQLReviewAgent | All SQL checks passed |
| `sql_review_agent:disallowed_keyword=<kw>` | SQLReviewAgent | DML/DDL keyword in LLM-generated SQL; retry triggered |
| `sql_review_agent:missing_from` | SQLReviewAgent | SQL missing FROM clause; retry triggered |
| `sql_review_agent:unknown_tables=[...]` | SQLReviewAgent | Referenced table not in DB; retry triggered |
| `sql_validator_agent:passed dialect=sqlite` | SQLValidatorAgent | AST validation passed |
| `sql_validator_agent:parse_error` | SQLValidatorAgent | sqlglot could not build an AST |
| `sql_validator_agent:disallowed_stmt_type=<type>` | SQLValidatorAgent | Non-SELECT AST statement blocked |
| `executor_agent:ok rows=N` | ExecutorAgent | Query executed; N rows returned |
| `executor_agent:error` | ExecutorAgent | SQL execution error |
| `clarify_node:terminal` | ClarifyNode | Pipeline ended at clarification node |
| `validation:empty_question` | ComplianceProcessor | No question in request |

---

## Error Responses

### 422 Unprocessable Entity

```json
{ "detail": [{ "loc": ["body","question"], "msg": "'question' must not be a blank string.", "type": "value_error" }] }
```

### 500 Internal Server Error

```json
{ "error": "Unexpected pipeline error: ..." }
```

### 503 Service Unavailable

```json
{ "detail": "Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds." }
```

---

     SECTION 5 — CONFIGURATION

# Part 5 · Configuration

All settings are managed by `config/settings.py` using [pydantic-settings](https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/concepts/pydantic_settings/). Settings are loaded from environment variables or a `.env` file in the project root.

---

## Application Settings

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `APPLICATION_NAME` | string | `COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)` | Human-readable name shown in logs |
| `HOST` | string | `0.0.0.0` | FastAPI server bind host |
| `PORT` | integer | `8000` | FastAPI server bind port |
| `DEBUG` | boolean | `false` | Enable uvicorn hot-reload |
| `LOG_LEVEL` | string | `INFO` | Root logging level |
| `LOG_DIR` | string | `logs` | Directory for rotating log files |

## Database Settings (`DATABASE__` prefix)

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DATABASE__DB_TYPE` | string | `SQLite` | Database engine type |
| `DATABASE__DB_FOLDER` | string | `data/comp_db` | Path to the folder containing `.db` files |
| `DATABASE__DB_NAME` | string | `COMP_REPORTS.db` | Default SQLite database filename |

## LLM Provider Settings (`MODEL__` prefix)

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MODEL__PROVIDER` | string | `dlh` | Active provider: `dlh` or `ica` |
| `MODEL__MODEL_NAME` | string | `google/gemma-4-31b-it` | Model name or ICA resource ID |
| `MODEL__MODEL_MAX_NEW_TOKENS` | integer | `512` | Maximum tokens per inference call |
| `MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE` | float | `0.1` | Sampling temperature |
| `MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL` | string | `http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1` | DLH endpoint |
| `MODEL__DLH_API_KEY` | string | `EMPTY` | DLH bearer token |
| `MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL` | boolean | `false` | TLS certificate verification |
| `MODEL__ICA_BASE_URL` | string | `https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com` | ICA API host |
| `MODEL__ICA_API_KEY` | string | _(empty)_ | ICA developer API key |
| `MODEL__ICA_NAMESPACE` | string | `chat-models` | ICA namespace |
| `MODEL__ICA_SEND_TEMPERATURE` | boolean | `false` | Forward temperature to ICA calls |

---

## Precedence Rules

1. Shell environment variables *(highest)*
2. `.env` file values
3. Default values in `config/settings.py` *(lowest)*

---

## Full `.env.example`

```env
# Application
APPLICATION_NAME="COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
PAGE_CONFIG="SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT"
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
LOG_DIR=logs
HOST=0.0.0.0
PORT=8000
DEBUG=false

# Database
DATABASE__DB_TYPE=SQLite
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/comp_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=COMP_REPORTS.db

# Model / LLM
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__MODEL_MAX_NEW_TOKENS=512
MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE=0.1
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL=false
MODEL__ICA_BASE_URL=https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com
MODEL__ICA_API_KEY=
MODEL__ICA_NAMESPACE=chat-models
MODEL__ICA_SEND_TEMPERATURE=false
```

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 6 — DATABASE SCHEMA
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->

# Part 6 · Database Schema

## Database File

| Property | Value |
|---|---|
| **File** | `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` |
| **Engine** | SQLite 3 |
| **Created by** | `data/seed_synthetic_data.py` |
| **Default table** | `COMP_TBL` |

---

## Table: COMP_TBL — Full Column Reference

| # | Column | SQLite Type | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `BRCD` | `TEXT` | Branch code |
| 2 | `CUST_NBR` | `TEXT` | Customer number |
| 3 | `CUST_NBR_CHKDGT` | `TEXT` | Customer number check digit |
| 4 | `ACCT_NBR` | `TEXT` | Account number |
| 5 | `ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT` | `TEXT` | Account number check digit |
| 6 | `ACCT_BRNCH` | `TEXT` | Account branch identifier |
| 7 | `BRTH_DT` | `TEXT` | Customer date of birth (`YYYY-MM-DD`) |
| 8 | `AGE_YRS` | `INTEGER` | Customer age in years |
| 9 | `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG` | `INTEGER` | Senior citizen flag: `1` = senior, `0` = not |
| 10 | `KYC_FLG` | `TEXT` | KYC status: `Y` = compliant, `N` = not |
| 11 | `END_OF_DAY_BAL` | `REAL` | End-of-day balance in INR |
| 12 | `ACCT_OPN_DT` | `TEXT` | Account opening date |
| 13 | `MTRTY_DT` | `TEXT` | Maturity date |
| 14 | `tenor_yr` | `INTEGER` | Tenor in years |
| 15 | `tenor_day` | `INTEGER` | Additional tenor days |
| 16 | `MAT_DT` | `TEXT` | Maturity date (may differ after modification) |
| 17 | `NEW_EFFCTV_RT` | `REAL` | New effective interest rate (%) |
| 18 | `PRDCT_ID` | `TEXT` | Product identifier code |
| 19 | `PF_FAC_REPT` | `TEXT` | PF facility flag: `Y` / `N` |
| 20 | `PRODUCT_NAME` | `TEXT` | Full product name |
| 21 | `BRANCHNAME` | `TEXT` | Full branch name |
| 22 | `CIRCLENAME` | `TEXT` | Circle name |
| 23 | `CIRCLECD` | `TEXT` | Circle code |
| 24 | `EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT` | `REAL` | Expected effective interest rate (%) |
| 25 | `ISMOD` | `TEXT` | Modified record flag: `YES` / `NO` |

### Compliance Flags

| Column | Values | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG` | `0`, `1` | Senior citizen account |
| `KYC_FLG` | `Y`, `N` | KYC compliance status |
| `PF_FAC_REPT` | `Y`, `N` | PF facility report |

---

## Inspecting the Database

```python
import sqlite3
conn = sqlite3.connect("data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db")
rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL").fetchall()
for row in rows:
    print(row)
conn.close()
```

```bash
sqlite3 data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db
sqlite> .tables
sqlite> .schema COMP_TBL
sqlite> SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL LIMIT 5;
```

# Part 7 · Synthetic Data

## Quick Start

```bash
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 1000          # append
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 5000 --reset --seed 99
```

## CLI Reference

| Flag | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--count` | `int` | `2000` | Number of rows to insert |
| `--reset` | flag | off | Delete all existing rows before inserting |
| `--db` | `Path` | `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` | Target SQLite file |
| `--seed` | `int` | `42` | Random seed for reproducibility |

---

## Generation Rules per Column

| Column | Rule |
|---|---|
| `BRCD` | Random 3-digit branch code `001`–`999` |
| `CUST_NBR` | Sequential `C000001`, `C000002`, … |
| `AGE_YRS` | Uniform random `18`–`85` |
| `SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG` | `1` if `AGE_YRS >= 60`, else `0` |
| `KYC_FLG` | `Y` with probability 80% for seniors, 90% for others |
| `END_OF_DAY_BAL` | Log-normal (mean ≈ ₹98k, range ₹10k–₹50L) |
| `tenor_yr` | Random from `{1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 15}` years |
| `MTRTY_DT` | `ACCT_OPN_DT + tenor` ±30 days if `ISMOD = YES` |
| `NEW_EFFCTV_RT` | Base rate ±0.75%; base `7.0%` for seniors, `6.5%` for others |
| `CIRCLENAME` / `CIRCLECD` | Random choice from 20 circle/code pairs across 13 cities |
| `ISMOD` | `YES` with probability 15% |

---

# Part 8 · UI Guide

## Overview of UI Options

| Option | File | Dependencies | Port | Auto-opens browser |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A — FastAPI HTML | `index.html` served by FastAPI | FastAPI, uvicorn | 8000 | No |
| B — Standalone Python | `run_ui.py` | None (stdlib only) | `CDSW_APP_PORT` (default 8100) | **Yes** |
| C — Gradio | `gradio_app.py` | `gradio>=4.36` | 7860 | **Yes** |

All three UIs connect to the same FastAPI backend (default `http://0.0.0.0:8066`, override via `CB_BACKEND_URL`).

### Standalone Python UI — Environment Variables

| Variable | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CDSW_APP_PORT` | *(required)* | Port the standalone UI server listens on |
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://0.0.0.0:8066` | FastAPI backend URL for API proxying |

---

## LLM Configuration Page

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| **Provider** | Dropdown: `DLH (on-prem)` or `ICA` |
| **Model / Resource ID** | Model name or ICA resource ID |
| **DLH Base URL** | DLH inference endpoint |
| **DLH API Key** | Bearer token for DLH |
| **ICA Base URL** | ICA API host URL |
| **ICA API Key** | ICA developer API key |
| **ICA Namespace** | `chat-models`, `agents`, `assistants`, or `digital-workforce` |

| Button | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Test & Save** | Calls `POST /api/v1/config/llm`, rebuilds LangGraph pipeline, saves to localStorage |
| **Load Saved Config** | Reads last saved config from localStorage and populates all fields |

---

## Backend Status LED

A pill widget in the **top-right corner of the topbar** shows live backend connectivity.

| State | LED colour | Label | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| Startup | Grey | `Checking…` | Initial state before first poll |
| Reachable (`GET /health` → 2xx) | Solid green | `Backend Active` | Static green dot |
| Unreachable | Blinking red | `Backend Offline` | 1.1 s blink animation |

- Polls every **8 seconds** (`setInterval`).
- Triggered immediately after a successful **Test & Save**.
- The proxy forwards `GET /health` from the UI server to the FastAPI backend.
- Broken-pipe / `ConnectionAbortedError` from slow LLM responses are silently swallowed (no traceback spam).

---

## Chat Page

### LangGraph Stage Pills

```
[ Intent Agent ]  [ Schema Agent ]  [ SQL Writer ]  [ SQL Review ]  [ Executor ]
```

Pills turn **blue** when the corresponding agent stage completes. A clarification pill appears when `intent_agent:clarification_required`.

### Composer Button Layout

```
[ Copy Chat ]  [ Export Chat ]  [ New Chat ]                           [ Send ]
←──────────────── left-aligned ───────────────────────────────────────→ ←right→
```

| Button | Action |
|---|---|
| **Copy Chat** | Copies plain-text conversation to clipboard |
| **Export Chat** | Downloads conversation as `conversation.txt` |
| **New Chat** | Clears chat, resets stage pills |
| **Send** | Submits question; disabled during query |

### Bot reply content

After a successful query the bot reply contains:
- **Agent trace** — compact stage string
- **Summary** — `result_summary` from the Executor Agent
- **SQL** — generated and reviewed query in a dark code block
- **Row count** and **full JSON result set**
- **↓ Download as CSV** — button to download result rows as `results_YYYY-MM-DD-HH-MM-SS.csv`

### Notice cards

| Situation | Card colour | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| Disallowed action word in question or LLM SQL | 🔴 Red blocked card | `intent_ok=False` with disallowed keyword, or `data.error` set |
| Vague/ambiguous question | 🔵 Blue clarification card | `needs_clarification: true` |

# Part 9 · Testing

## Running Tests

```bash
pytest                                             # all tests
pytest -v                                          # verbose
pytest tests/test_intent_agent.py -v               # single file
pytest tests/test_sql_review_agent.py::TestSQLReviewAgent::test_blocks_insert -v
pytest --cov=src --cov-report=term-missing         # with coverage
pytest --cov=src --cov-report=html                 # HTML report
```

## Test Configuration (`pyproject.toml`)

```toml
[tool.pytest.ini_options]
testpaths = ["tests"]
addopts = "--strict-markers -v --tb=short"
asyncio_mode = "auto"
```

---

## What to Test

| Module | What to test |
|---|---|
| `src/nl2sql_agents/intent.py` | Clear intent → `intent_ok=True`; vague → `intent_ok=False`; empty question; disallowed action word; too-short input; ambiguous check skipped when >3 tokens |
| `src/nl2sql_agents/schema.py` | DB selection scoring; schema Markdown output; missing db_path fallback |
| `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_writer_agent.py` | LLM called for every question (mocked); SQL extracted correctly; empty LLM output → `extraction_failed` stage; retry feedback included in prompt |
| `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_review.py` | DML keyword blocking (sets `error` field); missing SELECT; missing FROM; unknown tables; retry counter; max retries |
| `src/nl2sql_agents/sql_validator.py` | Valid SELECT passes; malformed SQL fails; INSERT blocked at AST; retry counter; SQL canonicalisation; dialect round-trip |
| `src/nl2sql_agents/executor.py` | Successful execution; empty result; SQL error; rollback |

---

## Agent Node Test Patterns

### IntentAgent

```python
from src.nl2sql_agents.intent import IntentAgent
from src.nl2sql_agents.state import AgentState


class TestIntentAgent:

    def setup_method(self):
        self.agent = IntentAgent()

    def test_clear_question_sets_intent_ok(self):
        state = AgentState(question="Show accounts where KYC flag is N", stages=[])
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True
        assert result["clarification_needed"] is None

    def test_vague_question_requests_clarification(self):
        state = AgentState(question="show", stages=[])
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert result["clarification_needed"] is not None

    def test_empty_question_returns_empty_stage(self):
        state = AgentState(question="", stages=[])
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert "intent_agent:empty" in result["stages"]

    def test_disallowed_action_word_blocked(self):
        state = AgentState(question="delete all accounts", stages=[])
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert any("disallowed_action_word" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_rich_question_skips_ambiguous_check(self):
        # >3 meaningful tokens — should NOT be caught by ambiguous check
        state = AgentState(
            question="How many people from kolkata branch having more than 70000?",
            stages=[],
        )
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True
```

### SQLWriterAgent (mocked LLM)

```python
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from src.nl2sql_agents.sql_writer_agent import SQLWriterAgent  # only LLM node
from src.nl2sql_agents.state import AgentState


class TestSQLWriterAgent:

    def setup_method(self):
        self.mock_llm = MagicMock()
        self.mock_llm.generate.return_value = (
            "<begin_sql_response>SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG='N';<end_sql_response>"
        )
        self.mock_llm.extract_sql.return_value = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG='N';"
        self.agent = SQLWriterAgent(model_service=self.mock_llm)

    def test_llm_called_for_every_question(self):
        state = AgentState(
            question="How many accounts in Mumbai circle?",
            interpreted_question="How many accounts in Mumbai circle?",
            schema_context="## Database: COMP_REPORTS\n### Table: COMP_TBL",
            stages=[],
        )
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_query"] == "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG='N';"
        self.mock_llm.generate.assert_called_once()

    def test_empty_llm_output_returns_extraction_failed_stage(self):
        self.mock_llm.extract_sql.return_value = ""
        state = AgentState(
            question="Show all accounts",
            interpreted_question="Show all accounts",
            schema_context="## Database: COMP_REPORTS\n### Table: COMP_TBL",
            stages=[],
        )
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert "sql_writer_agent:extraction_failed" in result["stages"]
```

### SQLValidatorAgent

```python
import pytest
from src.nl2sql_agents.sql_validator import SQLValidatorAgent
from src.nl2sql_agents.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture()
def agent() -> SQLValidatorAgent:
    return SQLValidatorAgent(dialect="sqlite")


class TestSQLValidatorAgent:

    def test_valid_select_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG='N';", retry_count=0, stages=[])
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is True

    def test_malformed_sql_fails(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE (KYC_FLG = 'N'", retry_count=0, stages=[])
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is False
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False

    def test_insert_blocked_at_ast_level(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES ('N', 60);", retry_count=0, stages=[])
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is False

    def test_retry_count_incremented_on_failure(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="NOT VALID SQL AT ALL !!!", retry_count=1, stages=[])
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["retry_count"] == 2
```

### ExecutorAgent (with real temp SQLite)

```python
import sqlite3
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from src.nl2sql_agents.executor import ExecutorAgent
from src.nl2sql_agents.state import AgentState


class TestExecutorAgent:

    def setup_method(self):
        self.agent = ExecutorAgent()
        tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".db", delete=False)
        self.db_path = Path(tmp.name)
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE COMP_TBL (KYC_FLG TEXT, AGE_YRS INTEGER)")
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES ('N', 64)")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def test_returns_rows(self):
        state = AgentState(
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG='N';",
            db_path=str(self.db_path),
            stages=[],
        )
        result = self.agent.run(state)
        assert len(result["result_rows"]) == 1
        assert result["result_rows"][0]["KYC_FLG"] == "N"
        assert result["result_columns"] == ["KYC_FLG", "AGE_YRS"]
        assert result.get("error") is None
```

# Part 10 · Deployment

## The Two `main.py` Files

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `launcher.py` | **Preferred entry-point** — starts backend + UI as sub-processes; watchdog monitors both |
| `main.py` (project root) | Alternative CLI entry-point — parses `--host`, `--port`, `--reload` and launches uvicorn |
| `src/main.py` | **FastAPI application** — defines `app`, all endpoints, lifespan |

**Preferred startup (both backend + UI together):**
```bash
python launcher.py
```

**Backend only:**
```bash
python main.py
# or
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000
```

Never run `python src/main.py` directly.

---

## Production — uvicorn

```bash
# Single worker
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --log-level info

# Multiple workers (recommended)
uvicorn src.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --workers 4 --log-level info

# With gunicorn
gunicorn src.main:app -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker -w 4 --bind 0.0.0.0:8000
```

---

## Docker

### Dockerfile

```dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt
COPY . .
EXPOSE 8000
CMD ["uvicorn", "src.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--log-level", "info"]
```

### docker-compose.yml

```yaml
version: "3.9"
services:
  compliance-bot:
    build: .
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    env_file:
      - .env
    volumes:
      - ./data:/app/data
      - ./logs:/app/logs
    restart: unless-stopped
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:8000/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3
```


---

## Log Management

```
logs/
├── compliance_bot.log        ← active file
├── compliance_bot.log.1      ← previous rotation
└── ...                       ← up to 10 backup files
```

Rotation: `RotatingFileHandler`, 10 MB per file, 10 backup files. Size-based (not time-based) because Windows cannot rename an open file at midnight.
