# Builds and returns the full Gradio Blocks application.

def build_app() -> gr.Blocks:
    initial_stages = _build_stage_html([])

    with gr.Blocks(title="Compliance Chatbot v2") as demo:

        # Brand header
        gr.HTML(
            """
            <div id="brand-header">
                <div id="brand-mark">CB</div>
                <div>
                    <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.2">Compliance Chatbot v2</div>
                    <div style="font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8">
                        LangGraph-powered agentic NL2SQL &mdash;
                        Intent &rarr; Schema &rarr; SQL Writer &rarr; SQL Review &rarr; Executor
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            """
        )

        # Tab 1: provider selector, DLH/ICA credential fields, Test & Save, Load Saved Config.
        with gr.Tab("⚙ LLM Configuration"):
            gr.Markdown(
                "### LLM Configuration\n"
                "Configure ICA or DLH and test the connection. "
                "The LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save."
            )

            with gr.Row():
                # Left column: provider selection form and action buttons.
                with gr.Column(scale=3):
                    provider_dd = gr.Dropdown(
                        label="Provider",
                        choices=["dlh", "ica"],
                        value="dlh",
                        interactive=True,
                    )
                    model_name_box = gr.Textbox(
                        label="Model / Resource ID",
                        value="google/gemma-4-31b-it",
                        interactive=True,
                    )

                    with gr.Group(visible=True) as dlh_group:
                        gr.Markdown("**DLH (on-prem) settings**")
                        dlh_base_url_box = gr.Textbox(
                            label="DLH Base URL",
                            value="http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
                            interactive=True,
                        )
                        dlh_api_key_box = gr.Textbox(
                            label="DLH API Key",
                            value="EMPTY",
                            interactive=True,
                            type="password",
                        )

                    with gr.Group(visible=False) as ica_group:
                        gr.Markdown("**ICA settings**")
                        ica_base_url_box = gr.Textbox(
                            label="ICA Base URL",
                            value="https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com",
                            interactive=True,
                        )
                        ica_api_key_box = gr.Textbox(
                            label="ICA API Key",
                            value="",
                            interactive=True,
                            type="password",
                        )
                        ica_namespace_dd = gr.Dropdown(
                            label="ICA Namespace",
                            choices=["chat-models", "agents", "assistants", "digital-workforce"],
                            value="chat-models",
                            interactive=True,
                        )

                    with gr.Row():
                        connect_btn = gr.Button("Test & Save", variant="primary")
                        load_btn = gr.Button("Load Saved Config", variant="secondary")

                    status_box = gr.Markdown("")

                # Right column: JSON preview of the last saved configuration.
                with gr.Column(scale=2):
                    gr.Markdown("### Saved Configuration")
                    gr.Markdown(
                        "Settings are stored locally so they remain available after restart.",
                        elem_classes=["panel-subtitle"],
                    )
                    config_preview = gr.Code(
                        label="",
                        value=_config_preview_text(_load_config()),
                        language="json",
                        interactive=False,
                        lines=20,
                    )

            # Collapsible activity log accordion.
            with gr.Accordion("Activity Log", open=False):
                log_box_cfg = gr.Textbox(
                    label="",
                    value="",
                    lines=4,
                    interactive=False,
                    elem_id="log-box",
                )

            # Show DLH fields or ICA fields depending on the selected provider.
            provider_dd.change(
                fn=_toggle_provider,
                inputs=[provider_dd],
                outputs=[dlh_group, ica_group],
            )

            # Wire the Test & Save button to _connect and update status + config preview.
            connect_btn.click(
                fn=_connect,
                inputs=[
                    provider_dd, model_name_box,
                    dlh_base_url_box, dlh_api_key_box,
                    ica_base_url_box, ica_api_key_box, ica_namespace_dd,
                ],
                outputs=[status_box, config_preview, log_box_cfg],
            )

            # Wire Load Saved Config button to restore all fields from the saved JSON.
            load_btn.click(
                fn=_load_config_ui,
                inputs=[],
                outputs=[
                    provider_dd, model_name_box,
                    dlh_base_url_box, dlh_api_key_box,
                    ica_base_url_box, ica_api_key_box, ica_namespace_dd,
                    dlh_group, ica_group,
                    config_preview, log_box_cfg,
                ],
            )

        # Tab 2: stage pills, chat window, message composer, action buttons.
        with gr.Tab("💬 Chat"):
            gr.Markdown(
                "### Conversational Chat\n"
                "Ask natural-language compliance questions. Five LangGraph agents collaborate: "
                "**Intent Agent** → **Schema Agent** → **SQL Writer** → **SQL Review** → **Executor**."
            )

            # Stage pills (HTML component, updated after each send)
            stage_html = gr.HTML(value=initial_stages, elem_id="stage-pills")

            # Raw bubble accumulator (never shown — feeds back into _send_message)
            raw_bubbles_state = gr.State("")

            # Chat window (displays the wrapped, scrollable HTML)
            chat_html = gr.HTML(value=_wrap_chat_html(""), elem_id="chat-window")

            # Message composer
            with gr.Row():
                with gr.Column(scale=5):
                    question_box = gr.Textbox(
                        label="Message",
                        placeholder=(
                            "Example: How many persons from Mumbai circle? "
                            "Press Enter to send, Shift+Enter for a new line."
                        ),
                        lines=3,
                        interactive=True,
                    )

            with gr.Row():
                send_btn = gr.Button("Send", variant="primary", scale=1)
                clear_btn = gr.Button("New Chat", variant="secondary", scale=1)
                attach_btn = gr.UploadButton(
                    "Attach File",
                    file_count="single",
                    variant="secondary",
                    scale=1,
                )
                copy_btn = gr.Button("Copy Chat", variant="secondary", scale=1)
                export_btn = gr.Button("Export Chat", variant="secondary", scale=1)

            # Attachment preview
            attach_preview = gr.Markdown("")
            # Hidden file path for download
            export_file = gr.File(label="Download Export", visible=False)

            # Shared attachment state
            attached_file_state = gr.State(None)

            # Activity log
            with gr.Accordion("Activity Log", open=False):
                log_box_chat = gr.Textbox(
                    label="",
                    value="",
                    lines=4,
                    interactive=False,
                    elem_id="log-box",
                )

            # Wire all event handlers: attach, send, clear, copy and export.

            # Attach file → update state + preview
            def _on_attach(file_obj):
                if file_obj is None:
                    return None, ""
                fname = Path(file_obj.name).name if hasattr(file_obj, "name") else str(file_obj)
                return file_obj, f"📎 **Attached:** {fname}"

            attach_btn.upload(
                fn=_on_attach,
                inputs=[attach_btn],
                outputs=[attached_file_state, attach_preview],
            )

            # Send message — raw_bubbles_state accumulates raw HTML; chat_html gets the wrapped display
            send_btn.click(
                fn=_send_message,
                inputs=[question_box, raw_bubbles_state, stage_html, attached_file_state],
                outputs=[raw_bubbles_state, chat_html, stage_html, question_box, attached_file_state, log_box_chat],
            )
            # Also send on Enter key (via submit)
            question_box.submit(
                fn=_send_message,
                inputs=[question_box, raw_bubbles_state, stage_html, attached_file_state],
                outputs=[raw_bubbles_state, chat_html, stage_html, question_box, attached_file_state, log_box_chat],
            )

            # Clear / New Chat — reset both raw state and display
            clear_btn.click(
                fn=_clear_chat,
                inputs=[],
                outputs=[raw_bubbles_state, chat_html, stage_html, question_box, attached_file_state, log_box_chat],
            )

            # Copy chat — write to a temp file then show as a text component
            copy_output = gr.Textbox(
                label="Chat Transcript (copy from here)",
                lines=6,
                interactive=False,
                visible=False,
            )

            def _copy_and_show(history_html: str):
                text = _copy_chat(history_html)
                return gr.update(value=text, visible=True)

            copy_btn.click(
                fn=_copy_and_show,
                inputs=[chat_html],
                outputs=[copy_output],
            )

            # Export chat
            def _export_and_show(history_html: str):
                path, log = _export_chat(history_html)
                return gr.update(value=path, visible=True), log

            export_btn.click(
                fn=_export_and_show,
                inputs=[chat_html],
                outputs=[export_file, log_box_chat],
            )

        # Centred footer rendered below all tabs.
        gr.HTML(
            """
            <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:24px;padding-top:12px;
                        border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8">
            </div>
            """
        )

    return demo

# Launch the Gradio app when the script is run directly.

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app = build_app()
    app.launch(
        server_name="0.0.0.0",
        server_port=int(os.getenv("CB_GRADIO_PORT", "7860")),
        share=False,
        css=CUSTOM_CSS,
        theme=gr.Theme(),
    )

                <input id="dlhApiKey" value="EMPTY" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="actions">
              <button class="primary" id="connectBtn">Test & Save</button>
              <button class="secondary" id="loadConfigBtn">Load Saved Config</button>
            </div>
            <div id="statusBox" class="status"></div>
          </section>

          <section class="panel">
            <h3>Saved Configuration</h3>
            <p class="panel-subtitle">Settings are stored locally so they remain available after refresh.</p>
            <div class="code-block" id="savedConfigPreview">No saved configuration yet.</div>
          </section>
        </section>

        <section class="page chat-page" data-page="chat">
          <section class="panel">
            <h3>Conversational Chat</h3>
            <p class="panel-subtitle">Ask compliance questions and continue the live conversation with markdown-style output.</p>

            <div class="stages">
              <div class="stage">Intent Analysis</div>
              <div class="stage">Clarification</div>
              <div class="stage">DB Selection</div>
              <div class="stage">SQL Generation</div>
              <div class="stage">Execution</div>
            </div>

            <div class="chat-window" id="chatWindow"></div>

            <div class="composer">
              <div class="field">
                <label for="questionInput">Message</label>
                <textarea id="questionInput" placeholder="Example: How many person from Mumbai circle? Press Enter to send, Shift+Enter for a new line."></textarea>
                <div class="hint">Press Enter to send. Use Shift + Enter to insert a new line.</div>
              </div>
              <div class="composer-buttons">
                <button class="secondary" id="copyChatBtn">Copy Chat</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="exportChatBtn">Export Chat</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="attachBtn">Attach File</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="clearBtn">New Chat</button>
                <span class="send-btn-wrap">
                  <button class="primary" id="sendBtn">Send</button>
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <input type="file" id="fileInput" class="hidden" />
            <div id="attachmentPreview"></div>
          </section>
        </section>

      </section>
    </main>
  </div>

  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const dlhFields = document.getElementById('dlhFields');
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
    const attachmentPreview = document.getElementById('attachmentPreview');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');
    const uiLogBox = document.getElementById('uiLogBox');

    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };

    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure providers, test connections, and persist runtime settings.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Use the configured provider for conversational compliance analysis.' }
    };

    let attachedFile = null;

    function logUi(message) {
      if (!uiLogBox) return;
      const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
      uiLogBox.textContent = `[${timestamp}] ${message}\n` + uiLogBox.textContent;
    }

    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }

    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }

    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }

    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);

      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }

      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;

      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }

    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      attachedFile = null;
      fileInput.value = '';
      attachmentPreview.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      logUi('Started a new chat session.');
    }

    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }

    async function connectProvider() {
      statusBox.className = 'status';
      statusBox.textContent = '';
      const payload = {
        provider: 'dlh',
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value
      };

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. You can start chatting now.`, 'bot');
        setActivePage('chat');
        logUi(`Provider connection successful: ${data.provider}.`);
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
        logUi(`Provider connection failed: ${error.message}`);
      }
    }

    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }

    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;

      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending…';
      showTypingIndicator();

      if (attachedFile) {
        addMessage(`Attached file: ${attachedFile.name}`, 'bot', '<div class="file-chip">📎 File attachment captured in UI only.</div>');
      }

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');

        const stagesHtml = (data.stages || [])
          .map(s => `<span class="stage">${s}</span>`)
          .join('');

        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addMessage(data.clarification_question, 'bot',
            `<strong>Stages</strong><div class="stages" style="margin-top:8px">${stagesHtml}</div>`);
          logUi('Assistant requested clarification.');
          return;
        }

        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        const summaryHtml = data.result_summary
          ? `<br><strong>Summary</strong><br>${data.result_summary}<br>`
          : '';
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Stages</strong><div class="stages" style="margin-top:8px;margin-bottom:12px">${stagesHtml}</div>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}${summaryHtml}<br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>`
        );
        logUi('Assistant completed SQL query execution.');
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(error.message, 'bot');
        logUi(`Chat execution failed: ${error.message}`);
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }

    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }

    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
      logUi('Current conversation copied to clipboard.');
    }

    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      logUi('Current conversation exported.');
    }

    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      loadConfigLocally();
      logUi('Loaded saved LLM configuration from local storage.');
    });
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);
    document.getElementById('attachBtn').addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
    fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
      attachedFile = fileInput.files[0] || null;
      attachmentPreview.innerHTML = attachedFile ? `<div class="file-chip">📎 ${attachedFile.name}</div>` : '';
      if (attachedFile) {
        logUi(`Attached file selected: ${attachedFile.name}`);
      }
    });

    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
      logUi('Sidebar collapse state changed.');
    });

    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });

    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        return;
      }
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
      logUi('Sidebar width resized.');
    });

    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
    logUi('UI initialized.');
  </script>
</body>
</html>



####################################################################################################
FILE: extra\launch_run_ui.py
####################################################################################################

"""
launch_run_ui.py — Launcher for the Compliance Chatbot HTML/JS UI server.

Usage
-----
    python launch_run_ui.py                     # defaults: UI=8090, backend=8000
    CB_UI_PORT=8091 python launch_run_ui.py     # custom UI port
    CB_BACKEND_PORT=9000 python launch_run_ui.py # custom backend port

What it does
------------
  • Owns ALL port and URL configuration for run_ui.py.
  • Builds CB_BACKEND_URL from CB_BACKEND_HOST + CB_BACKEND_PORT and forwards it
    to run_ui.py as an environment variable — run_ui.py has no hardcoded defaults.
  • Launches run_ui.py via ``python run_ui.py`` in the foreground.
  • Prints a clear summary of both the UI address and the backend proxy target
    before handing off to the child process.

Environment variables
---------------------
    CDSW_APP_PORT       UI port assigned by CML/CDSW at runtime (highest priority).
    CB_UI_PORT          UI port for local development (default: 8090).
    CB_BACKEND_HOST     FastAPI backend host (default: 127.0.0.1).
    CB_BACKEND_PORT     FastAPI backend port (default: 8000).
    CB_BACKEND_URL      Full backend URL — overrides host+port if set directly.
"""

import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


# ── UI port ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Priority: CDSW_APP_PORT (injected by CML) → CB_UI_PORT (manual) → 8090
UI_PORT: str = (
    os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT")
    or os.environ.get("CB_UI_PORT", "8090")
)

# ── Backend URL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Compose from host + port, unless CB_BACKEND_URL is set directly.
BACKEND_HOST: str = os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
BACKEND_PORT: str = os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_PORT", "8000")
BACKEND_URL: str = os.environ.get(
    "CB_BACKEND_URL",
    f"http://{BACKEND_HOST}:{BACKEND_PORT}",
)

# ── Path to run_ui.py ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
RUN_UI_FILE: str = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "run_ui.py")


def launch() -> None:
    """Forward port config to run_ui.py via env vars and start it."""

    port_source = (
        "CDSW_APP_PORT" if os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT")
        else "CB_UI_PORT" if os.environ.get("CB_UI_PORT")
        else "default"
    )
    backend_source = (
        "CB_BACKEND_URL" if os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_URL")
        else f"CB_BACKEND_HOST + CB_BACKEND_PORT ({BACKEND_HOST}:{BACKEND_PORT})"
    )

    print(f"\n{'─' * 58}")
    print(f"  Compliance Chatbot — HTML/JS UI launcher")
    print(f"{'─' * 58}")
    print(f"  Script      →  {RUN_UI_FILE}")
    print(f"  UI port     →  {UI_PORT}  (source: {port_source})")
    print(f"  UI address  →  http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}")
    print(f"  Backend     →  {BACKEND_URL}  (source: {backend_source})")
    print(f"{'─' * 58}\n")

    # Build the child environment — inherit everything, then inject our values.
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_UI_PORT"]      = UI_PORT       # run_ui.py reads CB_UI_PORT
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"]  = BACKEND_URL   # run_ui.py reads CB_BACKEND_URL
    # Keep CDSW_APP_PORT in env if present so run_ui.py CML detection still works.

    command = ["python", RUN_UI_FILE]

    try:
        subprocess.run(command, check=True, env=env)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopping the UI server...")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    launch()



####################################################################################################
FILE: extra\launch_ui.py
####################################################################################################

"""
launch_ui.py — Launcher for the Compliance Chatbot Streamlit UI.

Usage
-----
    python launch_ui.py                  # default host 127.0.0.1, port from env
    CB_UI_PORT=8502 python launch_ui.py  # override port via env var

What it does
------------
  • Resolves the absolute path to compliance_chatbot_ui/app.py.
  • Reads the bind port from CDSW_APP_PORT (CML/CDSW) → CB_UI_PORT → 8501.
  • Binds the server to 127.0.0.1 (loopback) so CML can proxy it safely.
  • Launches Streamlit via subprocess using the standard --server.* flags.

Environment variables
---------------------
    CDSW_APP_PORT   Port assigned by CML/CDSW at runtime (highest priority).
    CB_UI_PORT      Manual override for local development (default: 8501).
    CB_BACKEND_URL  Backend URL forwarded to the Streamlit process so the UI
                    can reach the FastAPI server (default: http://127.0.0.1:8000).
"""

import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


# ── Paths ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Absolute path to the Streamlit entry-point so the launcher works regardless
# of which directory the user runs it from.
APP_FILE: str = str(
    Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "compliance_chatbot_ui" / "app.py"
)

# ── Port resolution ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Priority: CDSW_APP_PORT (injected by CML) > CB_UI_PORT (manual) > 8501
PORT: str = os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT") or os.environ.get("CB_UI_PORT", "8501")

# ── Host ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Bind to loopback so the process is not exposed directly; CML reverse-proxies it.
ADDRESS: str = "127.0.0.1"

# ── Backend URL (forwarded to Streamlit as an env var) ─────────────────────────

BACKEND_URL: str = os.environ.get("CB_BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000")


def run_streamlit() -> None:
    """Build the Streamlit command and run it in the foreground."""

    command = [
        "streamlit",
        "run",
        APP_FILE,
        f"--server.address={ADDRESS}",
        f"--server.port={PORT}",
        "--server.headless=true",        # suppresses the email prompt in CML
        "--browser.gatherUsageStats=false",
    ]

    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"] = BACKEND_URL  # make sure the UI process sees the backend URL

    print(f"  Compliance Chatbot UI")
    print(f"  ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
    print(f"  App file   → {APP_FILE}")
    print(f"  Address    → http://{ADDRESS}:{PORT}")
    print(f"  Backend    → {BACKEND_URL}")
    print(f"  Port src   → {'CDSW_APP_PORT' if os.environ.get('CDSW_APP_PORT') else 'CB_UI_PORT / default'}")
    print(f"  ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n")

    try:
        subprocess.run(command, check=True, env=env)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopping the Streamlit server...")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_streamlit()



####################################################################################################
FILE: extra\run_ui.py
####################################################################################################

"""
run_ui.py — Self-contained UI server for Compliance Chatbot (LangGraph).

Intended to be launched via launch_run_ui.py which owns all port/URL config.
Can still be run directly; port and backend URL must be supplied via env vars:

    CB_UI_PORT=8090 CB_BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000 python run_ui.py

What it does:
  • Embeds the full single-page app as a Python string (no separate index.html needed).
  • Starts Python's built-in HTTP server on the port supplied by the launcher.
  • Proxies every /api/v1/... request to the FastAPI backend URL from the launcher.
  • Renders live LangGraph agent stage traces returned by /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate.
  • Opens the browser automatically (disabled in CML/CDSW environments).
No extra dependencies beyond the Python standard library.
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import threading
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import webbrowser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

# ── Port and backend URL ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# These values are injected by launch_run_ui.py via environment variables.
# No hardcoded defaults here — the launcher is the single source of truth.
# If run directly, CB_UI_PORT and CB_BACKEND_URL must be set in the environment.
_raw_port = os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT") or os.getenv("CB_UI_PORT")
if not _raw_port:
    print(
        "[run_ui] WARNING: Neither CDSW_APP_PORT nor CB_UI_PORT is set. "
        "Falling back to port 8090. Use launch_run_ui.py to set ports properly."
    )
    _raw_port = "8090"
UI_PORT: int = int(_raw_port)

_raw_backend = os.getenv("CB_BACKEND_URL")
if not _raw_backend:
    print(
        "[run_ui] WARNING: CB_BACKEND_URL is not set. "
        "Falling back to http://127.0.0.1:8000. Use launch_run_ui.py to set this properly."
    )
    _raw_backend = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
BACKEND_URL: str = _raw_backend.rstrip("/")

# Detect if running in CML/CDSW environment
IS_CML = os.getenv("CDSW_PROJECT") is not None

# Embedded HTML page — identical to index.html, fonts served from the local assets/ folder.
HTML = r"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Compliance Chatbot</title>
  <style>
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-Thin.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 100;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-ThinItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 100;
      font-style: italic;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-ExtraLight.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 200;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-ExtraLightItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 200;
      font-style: italic;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-Light.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 300;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-LightItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 300;
      font-style: italic;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-Regular.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 400;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("assets/fonts/Urbanist-Italic.ttf") format("truetype");

  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const dlhFields = document.getElementById('dlhFields');
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
    const attachmentPreview = document.getElementById('attachmentPreview');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');
    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };
    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure the LLM provider — the LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Five LangGraph agents: Intent → Schema → SQL Writer → SQL Review → Executor.' }
    };
    // Maps a LangGraph stage string prefix to the display name of the stage pill
    const STAGE_PREFIX_MAP = [
      { prefix: 'intent_agent',      label: 'Intent Agent' },
      { prefix: 'schema_agent',      label: 'Schema Agent' },
      { prefix: 'sql_writer_agent',  label: 'SQL Writer'   },
      { prefix: 'sql_review_agent',  label: 'SQL Review'   },
      { prefix: 'executor_agent',    label: 'Executor'     },
      { prefix: 'clarify_node',      label: 'Clarification'},
    ];
    /**
     * Highlight the stage pills that appear in the live LangGraph stage trace.
     * Each pill's data-prefix attribute is compared against every stage string
     * returned by the orchestrate endpoint; a match lights the pill up in accent blue.
     */
    function updateStagePills(stages) {
      const trail = document.getElementById('stageTrail');
      if (!trail) return;
      const pills = trail.querySelectorAll('.stage');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.style.background = '';
        pill.style.color = '';
        pill.style.borderColor = '';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '';
      });
      STAGE_PREFIX_MAP.forEach(({ prefix, label }, idx) => {
        const matched = stages.some(s => s.startsWith(prefix));
        if (!matched) return;
        const pill = pills[idx];
        if (!pill) return;
        pill.style.background = '#2563eb';
        pill.style.color = '#ffffff';
        pill.style.borderColor = '#1d4ed8';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '600';
      });
    }
    let attachedFile = null;
    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }
    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }
    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }
    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }
    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }
    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);
      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }
    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      attachedFile = null;
      fileInput.value = '';
      attachmentPreview.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      updateStagePills([]); // reset all pills to default (unlit) state
    }
    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }
    async function connectProvider() {
      statusBox.className = 'status';
      statusBox.textContent = '';
      const payload = {
        provider: 'dlh',
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value
      };
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. LangGraph pipeline is ready.`, 'bot');
        setActivePage('chat');
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
      }
    }
    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }
    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }
    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;
      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending…';
      showTypingIndicator();
      if (attachedFile) {
        addMessage(`Attached file: ${attachedFile.name}`, 'bot', '<div class="file-chip">📎 File attachment captured in UI only.</div>');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');
        // ---- Update stage pills with live LangGraph trace ----
        updateStagePills(data.stages || []);
        // Build a compact readable stage trace for the meta box
        const stageTrace = (data.stages || []).join(' → ');
        const stageHtml = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#57606a;margin-bottom:10px;word-break:break-all">${stageTrace || '—'}</div>`;
        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addMessage(data.clarification_question, 'bot',
            `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}`);
          return;
        }
        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const summary  = data.result_summary || `${rowCount} row(s) returned.`;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}` +
          `<strong>Summary</strong><br>${summary.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>')}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>`
        );
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(error.message, 'bot');
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }
    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }
    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
    }
    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }
    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', loadConfigLocally);
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);
    document.getElementById('attachBtn').addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
    fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
      attachedFile = fileInput.files[0] || null;
      attachmentPreview.innerHTML = attachedFile ? `<div class="file-chip">📎 ${attachedFile.name}</div>` : '';
    });
    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });
    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
    });
    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });
    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) return;
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
    });
    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""


# HTTP handler that serves the embedded HTML and proxies /api/* to the FastAPI backend.
class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Serve the UI and proxy /api/v1/* to the FastAPI backend."""

    # Silence the default per-request access log lines.
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    # Serve the HTML page, static assets and proxy GET API calls.
    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = HTML.encode("utf-8")
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body)
            return

        # Serve static assets (fonts/images) from the assets/ folder
        if path.startswith("/assets/"):
            assets_root = Path(__file__).parent / "assets"
            file_path = assets_root / path[len("/assets/") :]
            if file_path.is_file():
                data = file_path.read_bytes()
                ct = "application/octet-stream"
                if file_path.suffix in (".ttf", ".otf"):
                    ct = "font/truetype"
                elif file_path.suffix == ".css":
                    ct = "text/css"
                elif file_path.suffix in (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg"):
                    ct = f"image/{file_path.suffix.lstrip('.')}"
                self._send(200, ct, data)
            else:
                self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        # Proxy GET API calls (health probe etc.)
        if path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._proxy_get(path)
            return

        self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _proxy_get(self, path: str) -> None:
        try:
            url = BACKEND_URL + path
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)

    # Proxy all POST /api/* requests directly to the FastAPI backend.
    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        raw_body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
        target_url = BACKEND_URL + path
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            target_url,
            data=raw_body,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
            method="POST",
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            body = exc.read()
            self._send(exc.code, exc.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)


# Launch the server when the script is run directly.
def main() -> None:
    # Bind to 0.0.0.0 so CML can expose it
    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", UI_PORT), Handler)
    
    # Build the public URL for CML environment
    if IS_CML:
        cdsw_domain = os.getenv("CDSW_DOMAIN", "")
        engine_id = os.getenv("CDSW_ENGINE_ID", "")
        project_owner = os.getenv("PROJECT_OWNER", "")
        project_name = os.getenv("CDSW_PROJECT", "")
        
        if cdsw_domain and engine_id:
            # CML app URL format
            public_url = f"https://{engine_id}.{cdsw_domain}"
            print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
            print(f"  Compliance Chatbot UI")
            print(f"{'=' * 60}")
            print(f"  Public URL  →  {public_url}")
            print(f"  Local URL   →  http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}")
            print(f"  Backend     →  {BACKEND_URL}")
            print(f"  Port        →  {UI_PORT} (CDSW_APP_PORT)")
            print(f"{'=' * 60}")
            print("  App is running. Click the URL above to open in CML.\n")
        else:
            print(f"\n  Compliance Chatbot UI  →  http://0.0.0.0:{UI_PORT}")
            print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
            print("  Running in CML environment\n")
    else:
        url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}"
        print(f"\n  Compliance Chatbot UI  →  {url}")
        print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
        print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")
        # Only open browser when NOT in CML
        threading.Timer(0.8, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# ─── Session state ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _load_saved_config() -> dict:
    if SAVED_CONFIG_FILE.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(SAVED_CONFIG_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
            pass
    return {
        "provider": "dlh",
        "model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
        "dlh_base_url": "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
        "dlh_api_key": "EMPTY",
        "dlh_verify_ssl": False,
        "ica_base_url": "https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com",
        "ica_api_key": "",
        "ica_namespace": "chat-models",
        "ica_send_temperature": False,
    }


def _save_config(cfg: dict) -> None:
    try:
        SAVED_CONFIG_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(cfg, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        pass


for _k, _v in {
    "page": "llm_config",
    "messages": [],
    "llm_config": _load_saved_config(),
    "backend_status": None,
    "active_stages": [],
}.items():
    if _k not in st.session_state:
        st.session_state[_k] = _v

# ─── API helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _check_health() -> bool:
    try:
        r = requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}/health", timeout=5)
        return r.status_code == 200
    except requests.RequestException:
        return False


def _configure_llm(cfg: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        r = requests.post(
            f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/v1/config/llm",
            json={
                "provider": cfg["provider"],
                "model_name": cfg.get("model_name"),
                "dlh_base_url": cfg.get("dlh_base_url"),
                "dlh_api_key": cfg.get("dlh_api_key"),
                "dlh_verify_ssl": cfg.get("dlh_verify_ssl", False),
                "ica_base_url": cfg.get("ica_base_url"),
                "ica_api_key": cfg.get("ica_api_key"),
                "ica_namespace": cfg.get("ica_namespace"),
                "ica_send_temperature": cfg.get("ica_send_temperature", False),
            },
            timeout=30,
        )
        data = r.json()
        if r.status_code == 200 and data.get("success"):
            return True, data.get("message", "LLM connection successful.")
        return False, data.get("detail") or data.get("message") or "Connection failed."
    except requests.RequestException as exc:
        return False, f"Request error: {exc}"


def _orchestrate(question: str) -> dict:
    r = requests.post(
        f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
        json={"question": question, "question_id": str(uuid.uuid4())},
        timeout=120,
    )
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()

# ─── HTML builders ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _stage_pills_html(completed: list[str]) -> str:
    pills = "".join(
        f'<span class="pill {"done" if any(k in s for s in completed) else ""}">{lbl}</span>'
        for k, lbl in STAGE_PILLS
    )
    return f'<div class="stage-pills">{pills}</div>'


def _table_html(columns: list[str], rows: list[dict]) -> str:
    if not rows or not columns:
        return ""
    ths = "".join(f"<th>{c}</th>" for c in columns)
    trs = ""
    for row in rows[:100]:
        tds = "".join(
            f"<td title='{str(row.get(c, '')).replace(chr(39), '')}'>{ row.get(c, '')}</td>"
            for c in columns
        )
        trs += f"<tr>{tds}</tr>"
    extra = (
        f'<tr><td colspan="{len(columns)}" class="more-rows">'
        f'… {len(rows) - 100} more rows not shown</td></tr>'
        if len(rows) > 100 else ""
    )
    return (
        f'<div class="result-wrap">'
        f'<table class="result-tbl"><thead><tr>{ths}</tr></thead>'
        f'<tbody>{trs}{extra}</tbody></table></div>'
    )


def _message_html(msg: dict) -> str:
    """Return the full HTML for one chat message (pure HTML, no Streamlit widgets)."""
    if msg["role"] == "user":
        text = msg.get("content", "").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")
        return f'<div class="msg-row-user"><div class="bubble-user">{text}</div></div>'

    stages   = msg.get("stages", [])
    sql      = msg.get("sql") or ""
    rows     = msg.get("rows") or []
    cols     = msg.get("columns") or []
    summary  = msg.get("summary") or ""
    error    = msg.get("error") or ""
    needs_cl = msg.get("needs_clarification", False)
    cl_q     = msg.get("clarification_question") or ""

    inner = _stage_pills_html(stages) if stages else ""

    if error:
        inner += f'<div class="bot-error">⚠ {error}</div>'
    elif needs_cl and cl_q:
        inner += f'<div class="bot-clarify"><strong>Clarification needed:</strong> {cl_q}</div>'
    else:
        if summary:
            inner += f'<p class="bot-summary">{summary}</p>'
        if sql:
            safe_sql = sql.replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")
            inner += f"<pre>{safe_sql}</pre>"
        if rows and cols:
            inner += _table_html(cols, rows)
        if not inner.strip():
            inner += '<span style="color:#94a3b8;">No results returned.</span>'

    return (
        f'<div class="msg-row-bot">'
        f'<div class="bot-avatar">CB</div>'
        f'<div class="bubble-bot">{inner}</div>'
        f'</div>'
    )


def _build_chat_viewport_html() -> str:
    msgs = st.session_state.messages
    if not msgs:
        body = (
            '<div class="chat-empty">'
            '<div style="font-size:2rem;margin-bottom:0.4rem;">🤖</div>'
            '<div style="font-size:0.9rem;">Ask a compliance question to get started.</div>'
            '<div style="font-size:0.78rem;font-style:italic;color:#b0bec5;margin-top:4px;">'
            'Example: How many senior citizens are in the Thane branch?'
            '</div>'
            '</div>'
        )
    else:
        body = "\n".join(_message_html(m) for m in msgs)
    return f'<div class="chat-viewport" id="cbViewport">{body}</div>'


def _export_chat() -> str:
    lines = [
        f"Compliance Chatbot — Chat Export ({datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')})",
        "=" * 60,
    ]
    for msg in st.session_state.messages:
        if msg["role"] == "user":
            lines.append(f"\n[You] {msg['content']}")
        else:
            lines.append("\n[Bot]")
            if msg.get("summary"):  lines.append(f"  Summary : {msg['summary']}")
            if msg.get("sql"):      lines.append(f"  SQL     : {msg['sql']}")
            if msg.get("stages"):   lines.append(f"  Stages  : {', '.join(msg['stages'])}")
            if msg.get("error"):    lines.append(f"  Error   : {msg['error']}")
    return "\n".join(lines)

                <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:0.97rem;color:#f1f5f9;
                            line-height:1.25;margin-bottom:2px;">Compliance Chatbot</div>

                    Enterprise ICA and DLH powered<br>compliance workspace.

        st.session_state.page = "llm_config"
        st.rerun()

    # ── Chat nav item ──
    chat_active = st.session_state.page == "chat"
    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <div class="nav-card {'active' if chat_active else ''}">
            <div class="nav-icon">💬</div>
            <div>
                <div class="nav-text-title">Chat</div>
                <div class="nav-text-sub">Conversation and markdown-style responses</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )
    if st.button("nav_chat_btn", key="nav_chat", use_container_width=True):
        st.session_state.page = "chat"
        st.rerun()

    st.markdown('<hr class="sb-divider">', unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # ── Backend status ──
    col_s, col_r = st.columns([3, 1])
    with col_s:
        if st.session_state.backend_status is True:
            st.markdown(
                '<span style="color:#4ade80;font-size:0.78rem;">● Backend online</span>',
                unsafe_allow_html=True,
            )
        elif st.session_state.backend_status is False:
            st.markdown(
                '<span style="color:#f87171;font-size:0.78rem;">● Backend offline</span>',
                unsafe_allow_html=True,

        else:

                '<span style="color:#64748b;font-size:0.78rem;">○ Status unknown</span>',
                unsafe_allow_html=True,
            )
    with col_r:
        if st.button("↺", key="btn_health", help="Check backend health"):
