            st.rerun()

    st.markdown(
        f'<div style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#475569;margin-top:2px;word-break:break-all;">'
        f'{BACKEND_URL}</div>',
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

# ─── Page: LLM Configuration ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _page_llm_config() -> None:
    st.markdown('<div class="page-h1">LLM Configuration</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    st.markdown(
        '<div class="page-sub">Configure providers, test connections, and persist runtime settings.</div>',
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    cfg = st.session_state.llm_config
    col_form, col_saved = st.columns([5, 7], gap="large")

    # ── Left: configuration form card ──
    with col_form:
        st.markdown(
            """
            <div class="cb-card">
                <div class="cb-card-title">LLM Configuration</div>
                <div class="cb-card-sub">
                    Configure ICA or DLH and persist settings locally between sessions.
                </div>
            """,
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )

        # Provider dropdown — pretty labels
        provider_options = {"dlh": "DLH (on-prem)", "ica": "ICA (IBM)"}
        provider_keys    = list(provider_options.keys())
        provider_labels  = list(provider_options.values())
        current_idx      = provider_keys.index(cfg.get("provider", "dlh"))

        st.markdown('<div class="field-label">Provider</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
        provider_label = st.selectbox(
            label="",
            options=provider_labels,
            index=current_idx,
            key="cfg_provider",
            label_visibility="collapsed",
        )
        provider = provider_keys[provider_labels.index(provider_label)]

        st.markdown('<div class="field-label">Model / Resource ID</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
        model_name = st.text_input(
            label="",
            value=cfg.get("model_name", ""),
            key="cfg_model_name",
            placeholder="e.g. google/gemma-4-31b-it",
            label_visibility="collapsed",
        )

        if provider == "dlh":
            st.markdown('<div class="field-label">DLH Base URL</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            dlh_base_url = st.text_input(
                label="",
                value=cfg.get("dlh_base_url", ""),
                key="cfg_dlh_url",
                placeholder="http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
                label_visibility="collapsed",
            )
            st.markdown('<div class="field-label">DLH API Key</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            dlh_api_key = st.text_input(
                label="",
                value=cfg.get("dlh_api_key", "EMPTY"),
                key="cfg_dlh_key",
                type="default",          # shown as plain text matching screenshot
                label_visibility="collapsed",
            )
            dlh_verify_ssl  = cfg.get("dlh_verify_ssl", False)
            ica_base_url    = cfg.get("ica_base_url", "")
            ica_api_key     = cfg.get("ica_api_key", "")
            ica_namespace   = cfg.get("ica_namespace", "chat-models")
            ica_send_temp   = cfg.get("ica_send_temperature", False)
        else:
            dlh_base_url   = cfg.get("dlh_base_url", "")
            dlh_api_key    = cfg.get("dlh_api_key", "EMPTY")
            dlh_verify_ssl = cfg.get("dlh_verify_ssl", False)

            st.markdown('<div class="field-label">ICA Base URL</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            ica_base_url = st.text_input(
                label="",
                value=cfg.get("ica_base_url", "https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com"),
                key="cfg_ica_url",
                label_visibility="collapsed",
            )
            st.markdown('<div class="field-label">ICA API Key</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            ica_api_key = st.text_input(
                label="",
                value=cfg.get("ica_api_key", ""),
                key="cfg_ica_key",
                type="password",
                placeholder="sk-…",
                label_visibility="collapsed",
            )
            ns_idx = ICA_NAMESPACES.index(cfg.get("ica_namespace", "chat-models")) \
                     if cfg.get("ica_namespace") in ICA_NAMESPACES else 0
            st.markdown('<div class="field-label">ICA Namespace</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            ica_namespace = st.selectbox(
                label="",
                options=ICA_NAMESPACES,
                index=ns_idx,
                key="cfg_ica_ns",
                label_visibility="collapsed",
            )
            ica_send_temp = st.checkbox(
                "Send temperature / max_tokens to ICA",
                value=cfg.get("ica_send_temperature", False),
                key="cfg_ica_temp",
            )

        st.markdown("<div style='margin-top:0.5rem;'></div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

        btn_col1, btn_col2 = st.columns([1, 1], gap="small")
        with btn_col1:
            save_clicked = st.button(
                "Test & Save",
                type="primary",
                use_container_width=True,
                key="btn_save",
            )
        with btn_col2:
            load_clicked = st.button(
                "Load Saved Config",
                use_container_width=True,
                key="btn_load",
            )

        st.markdown("</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)   # close cb-card

        if save_clicked:
            new_cfg: dict[str, Any] = {
                "provider":            provider,
                "model_name":          model_name,
                "dlh_base_url":        dlh_base_url,
                "dlh_api_key":         dlh_api_key,
                "dlh_verify_ssl":      dlh_verify_ssl,
                "ica_base_url":        ica_base_url,
                "ica_api_key":         ica_api_key,
                "ica_namespace":       ica_namespace,
                "ica_send_temperature": ica_send_temp,
            }
            with st.spinner("Testing LLM connection and rebuilding LangGraph pipeline…"):
                ok, msg = _configure_llm(new_cfg)
            if ok:
                st.session_state.llm_config = new_cfg
                _save_config(new_cfg)
                st.success(f"✓ {msg}")
            else:
                st.error(f"✗ {msg}")

        if load_clicked:
            st.session_state.llm_config = _load_saved_config()
            st.success("Loaded saved configuration.")
            st.rerun()

    # ── Right: saved configuration card ──
    with col_saved:
        st.markdown(
            """
            <div class="cb-card">
                <div class="cb-card-title">Saved Configuration</div>
                <div class="cb-card-sub">
                    Settings are stored locally so they
                    <span class="highlight">remain</span> available after refresh.
                </div>
            """,
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )
        disp = dict(st.session_state.llm_config)
        if not disp:
            code_content = "No saved configuration yet."
        else:
            if disp.get("ica_api_key"):
                k = disp["ica_api_key"]
                disp["ica_api_key"] = k[:8] + "…" if len(k) > 8 else "***"
            if disp.get("dlh_api_key") and disp["dlh_api_key"] != "EMPTY":
                disp["dlh_api_key"] = "***"
            code_content = json.dumps(disp, indent=2)
        st.markdown(
            f'<div class="config-block">{code_content}</div>',
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )
        st.markdown("</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)   # close cb-card


# ─── Page: Chat ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _page_chat() -> None:
    st.markdown('<div class="page-h1">Chat</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    st.markdown(
        '<div class="page-sub blue">'
        'Use the configured provider for conversational compliance analysis.'
        '</div>',
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    # ── Chat card ──
    st.markdown(
        """
        <div class="cb-card">
            <div class="cb-card-title">Conversational Chat</div>
            <div class="cb-card-sub">
                Ask compliance questions and continue the
                <span class="highlight">live conversation</span>
                with markdown-style output.
            </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    # Stage pills
    st.markdown(_stage_pills_html(st.session_state.active_stages), unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # Chat viewport — entire message history as one HTML block
    st.markdown(_build_chat_viewport_html(), unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # close cb-card
    st.markdown("</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # ── Message input section (below card) ──
    st.markdown(
        '<div style="margin-top:1.1rem;">'
        '<div class="msg-label">Message</div>',
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    with st.form(key="chat_form", clear_on_submit=True):
        question = st.text_area(
            label="",
            placeholder="Example: How many person from Mumbai circle? Press Enter to send, Shift+Enter for a new line.",
            height=95,
            key="chat_input",
            label_visibility="collapsed",
        )
        st.caption("Press Enter to send. Use Shift + Enter to insert a new line.")

        # Button row: left group + spacer + Send (right)
        c_copy, c_export, c_attach, c_new, c_gap, c_send = st.columns(
            [1.05, 1.2, 1.05, 1.0, 3.5, 0.85]
        )
        with c_copy:
            copy_btn   = st.form_submit_button("Copy Chat",   use_container_width=True)
        with c_export:
            export_btn = st.form_submit_button("Export Chat", use_container_width=True)
        with c_attach:
            attach_btn = st.form_submit_button("Attach File", use_container_width=True)
        with c_new:
            new_btn    = st.form_submit_button("New Chat",    use_container_width=True)
        with c_gap:
            st.empty()
        with c_send:
            st.markdown('<div class="send-btn">', unsafe_allow_html=True)
            send_btn = st.form_submit_button("Send", type="primary", use_container_width=True)
            st.markdown("</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    st.markdown("</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # ── Form action handlers ──
    if new_btn:
        st.session_state.messages = []
        st.session_state.active_stages = []
        st.rerun()

    if copy_btn and st.session_state.messages:
        st.info("Use Export Chat to download the full conversation as a text file.")

    if export_btn and st.session_state.messages:
        st.download_button(
            label="⬇ Download chat.txt",
            data=_export_chat(),
            file_name=f"compliance_chat_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M')}.txt",
            mime="text/plain",
            key="dl_btn",
        )

    if attach_btn:
        st.info("File attachment is UI-only — the backend does not process file uploads.")

    if send_btn:
        if not (question and question.strip()):
            st.warning("Please enter a question before sending.")
        else:
            q = question.strip()
            st.session_state.messages.append({"role": "user", "content": q})
            st.session_state.active_stages = []

            with st.spinner("Running LangGraph pipeline…"):
                try:
                    resp   = _orchestrate(q)
                    stages = resp.get("stages", [])
                    st.session_state.active_stages = stages
                    st.session_state.messages.append({
                        "role":                   "bot",
                        "content":                resp.get("result_summary") or "",
                        "stages":                 stages,
                        "sql":                    resp.get("sql_query"),
                        "rows":                   resp.get("df_output") or [],
                        "columns":                resp.get("result_columns") or [],
                        "summary":                resp.get("result_summary"),
                        "needs_clarification":    resp.get("needs_clarification", False),
                        "clarification_question": resp.get("clarification_question"),
                        "error":                  resp.get("error"),
                    })
                except requests.RequestException as exc:
                    st.session_state.messages.append({
                        "role": "bot", "content": "", "stages": [],
                        "sql": None, "rows": [], "columns": [], "summary": None,
                        "needs_clarification": False, "clarification_question": None,
                        "error": f"Backend error: {exc}",
                    })
            st.rerun()

# ─── Router ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if st.session_state.page == "llm_config":
    _page_llm_config()
else:
    _page_chat()



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\__init__.py
####################################################################################################

"""Compliance Chatbot — src package."""



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\main.py
####################################################################################################

"""
FastAPI application entry-point for the Compliance Chatbot v2.

Wires together:
  - LangGraph NL2SQL agentic pipeline (5 agent nodes)
  - ComplianceProcessor (maps AgentState ↔ API schemas)
  - Static assets and Gradio-compatible CORS

Endpoints:
  GET  /health                        — liveness probe
  POST /api/v1/config/llm             — configure & test LLM connection
  POST /api/v1/compliance/chat        — simplified NL-to-SQL chat response
  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate — full agentic orchestration response

Pipeline nodes (in order):
  1. IntentAgent (intent.py)         — rule-based intent classifier, no LLM
  2. SchemaAgent (schema.py)         — live SQLite schema introspection, no LLM
  3. SQLWriterAgent (sql_writer_agent.py) — LLM SQL generation — only LLM call
  4. SQLReviewAgent (sql_review.py)  — regex safety + table-existence check, no LLM
  4.5 SQLValidatorAgent (sql_validator.py) — sqlglot AST validation, no LLM
  5. ExecutorAgent (executor.py)     — read-only SQLite execution, no LLM

Note: database seeding is not automatic.
Run ``python data/seed_synthetic_data.py`` to populate the database.
"""

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncGenerator

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from config.settings import ModelSettings, get_settings
from src.business_logic.compliance_processor import ComplianceProcessor
from src.exceptions.custom_exceptions import ComplianceBotError
from src.models.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    HealthResponse,
    LLMConnectionRequest,
    LLMConnectionResponse,
)
from src.nl2sql_agents.graph import build_nl2sql_graph
from src.services.model_loader_service import ModelLoaderService
from src.utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.main")

_model_service: ModelLoaderService | None = None
_compliance_processor: ComplianceProcessor | None = None

# Builds ModelLoaderService, LangGraph pipeline and ComplianceProcessor together.

def _build_processor(model_settings: ModelSettings) -> tuple[ModelLoaderService, ComplianceProcessor]:
    """
    Construct a ``ModelLoaderService``, build the LangGraph pipeline, and
    return both the service and the compliance processor.

    Args:
        model_settings: LLM configuration (provider, API key, base URL …).

    Returns:
        Tuple of ``(ModelLoaderService, ComplianceProcessor)``.
    """
    model_service = ModelLoaderService(settings=model_settings)
    model_service.load()

    db_path = get_settings().database.db_path
    compiled_graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
        model_service=model_service,
        db_path=db_path,
    )
    processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=compiled_graph)
    return model_service, processor

# FastAPI lifespan: starts the LLM + LangGraph pipeline on startup, shuts down on exit.

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None, None]:
    """FastAPI lifespan handler — startup / shutdown."""
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement
    settings = get_settings()
    LOGGER.info("Starting %s …", settings.application_name)

    try:
        _model_service, _compliance_processor = _build_processor(settings.model)
    except ComplianceBotError as exc:
        LOGGER.error("Model loading failed — API will return 503 on inference: %s", exc)

    yield

    LOGGER.info("Shutting down %s …", settings.application_name)
    if _model_service and _model_service.is_loaded():
        _model_service.unload()

# FastAPI app instance with CORS, static-file mount and lifespan wired in.

settings = get_settings()
app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.application_name,
    description=(
        "LangGraph-based agentic NL-to-SQL compliance query engine for banking "
        "term-deposit products.  Five specialised agents collaborate to analyse "
        "intent, retrieve schema, generate, review, and execute SQL queries."
    ),
    version="2.0.0",
    docs_url="/docs",
    redoc_url="/redoc",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

app.mount(
    "/assets",
    StaticFiles(directory=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "assets"),
    name="assets",
)

# Maps ComplianceBotError domain exceptions to structured 500 JSON responses.

@app.exception_handler(ComplianceBotError)
async def compliance_bot_exception_handler(
    _request: Request,
    exc: ComplianceBotError,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """Map domain errors to a structured 500 JSON response."""
    LOGGER.error("Domain error: %s", exc.message)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        content={"error": exc.message},
    )

# API route handlers: health, LLM config, chat and orchestrate endpoints.

@app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
async def index() -> FileResponse:
    """Serve the single-page application shell."""
    return FileResponse(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "index.html")

@app.get(
    "/health",
    response_model=HealthResponse,
    summary="Liveness probe",
    tags=["Infrastructure"],
)
async def health_check() -> HealthResponse:
    """Return service health status."""
    return HealthResponse(status="ok", version="2.0.0")

@app.post(
    "/api/v1/config/llm",
    response_model=LLMConnectionResponse,
    tags=["Configuration"],
    summary="Configure and test LLM connection",
)
async def configure_llm(request: LLMConnectionRequest) -> LLMConnectionResponse:
    """
    Reconfigure the active LLM provider and rebuild the LangGraph pipeline.

    Args:
        request: Provider settings to apply.

    Returns:
        Connection test result.
    """
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    base_settings = get_settings().model.model_copy(deep=True)
    candidate_settings = base_settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "provider": request.provider,
            "model_name": request.model_name or base_settings.model_name,
            "dlh_base_url": request.dlh_base_url or base_settings.dlh_base_url,
            "dlh_api_key": request.dlh_api_key or base_settings.dlh_api_key,
            "dlh_verify_ssl": request.dlh_verify_ssl,
            "ica_base_url": request.ica_base_url or base_settings.ica_base_url,
            "ica_api_key": request.ica_api_key or base_settings.ica_api_key,
            "ica_namespace": request.ica_namespace or base_settings.ica_namespace,
            "ica_send_temperature": request.ica_send_temperature,
        }
    )

    model_service, processor = _build_processor(candidate_settings)
    model_service.test_connection()
    _model_service = model_service
    _compliance_processor = processor

    return LLMConnectionResponse(
        success=True,
        provider=candidate_settings.provider,
        message="LLM connection successful. LangGraph pipeline is ready.",
    )

@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/chat",
    response_model=ComplianceChatResponse,
    summary="Natural-language compliance query (simplified response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_chat(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceChatResponse:
    """
    Run the NL2SQL agentic pipeline and return a simplified response.

    Args:
        request: Natural-language compliance question.

    Returns:
        Generated SQL and result rows.
    """
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    LOGGER.info("Received chat request — question_id=%s", request.question_id)
    return _compliance_processor.process_query(request)

@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
    response_model=ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    summary="Agentic NL2SQL compliance query (full orchestration response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_orchestrate(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
    """
    Run the full LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline and return agent-level detail.

    The response includes all completed stage names, the interpreted
    question, the generated SQL, result rows, and any clarification
    request produced by the Intent Agent.

    Args:
        request: Natural-language compliance question.

    Returns:
        Full orchestration response with stage trace.
    """
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    return _compliance_processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import uvicorn

    _settings = get_settings()
    uvicorn.run(
        "src.main:app",
        host=_settings.host,
        port=_settings.port,
        reload=_settings.debug,
        log_level=_settings.log_level.lower(),
    )



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\business_logic\__init__.py
####################################################################################################

"""Compliance Chatbot — business logic package."""



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\business_logic\compliance_processor.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Compliance Processor — core business-logic orchestration layer.

Single integration point between the FastAPI layer and the LangGraph NL2SQL
agentic pipeline.  Accepts a ``ComplianceChatRequest``, invokes the compiled
graph, and maps the resulting ``AgentState`` onto the Pydantic response models.

Architecture
------------

    FastAPI endpoint
        └─► ComplianceProcessor.process_query_with_orchestration()
                └─► compiled_graph.invoke(AgentState)
                        ├─► IntentAgent      (intent.py       — rule-based, no LLM)
                        ├─► SchemaAgent      (schema.py       — SQLite PRAGMA, no LLM)
                        ├─► SQLWriterAgent   (sql_writer_agent.py — LLM call ← only one)
                        ├─► SQLReviewAgent   (sql_review.py   — regex + SQLite, no LLM)
                        ├─► SQLValidatorAgent(sql_validator.py— sqlglot AST, no LLM)
                        └─► ExecutorAgent    (executor.py     — SQLite execute, no LLM)
"""

import uuid
from typing import Any

from src.exceptions.custom_exceptions import ComplianceBotError
from src.models.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
)
from src.nl2sql_agents.state import AgentState
from src.utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.business_logic.compliance_processor")


class ComplianceProcessor:
    """
    Orchestrates the NL2SQL compliance query pipeline via LangGraph.

    This class is intentionally thin.  All multi-agent logic lives inside
    the individual agent nodes and the graph wiring.  The processor maps
    between API request/response models and the ``AgentState`` dict.

    Args:
        compiled_graph: A compiled LangGraph ``StateGraph`` returned by
                        ``build_nl2sql_graph()``.
    """

    def __init__(self, compiled_graph: Any) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the processor.

        Args:
            compiled_graph: Compiled LangGraph pipeline from
                            ``src.nl2sql_agents.graph.build_nl2sql_graph``.
        """
        self._graph = compiled_graph

    # Public API — exposed to the FastAPI layer.

    def process_query(self, request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceChatResponse:
        """
        Run the agentic pipeline and return the simplified chat response.

        Args:
            request: Validated inbound API request.

        Returns:
            ``ComplianceChatResponse`` with SQL and result rows (or error).
        """
        orchestration = self.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        return ComplianceChatResponse(
            question_id=orchestration.question_id,
            sql_query=orchestration.sql_query,
            df_output=orchestration.df_output,
            result_columns=orchestration.result_columns,
            result_summary=orchestration.result_summary,
            error=orchestration.error or orchestration.clarification_question,
        )

    def process_query_with_orchestration(
        self,
        request: ComplianceChatRequest,
    ) -> ComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
        """
        Run the full LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline and return the detailed response.

        Args:
            request: Validated inbound API request.

        Returns:
            ``ComplianceOrchestrationResponse`` with all agent stages, generated
            SQL, result rows, and any clarification or error detail.

        Raises:
            ComplianceBotError: Propagated from within the graph on domain errors.
        """
        question_id = request.question_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        question = (request.question or "").strip()

        if not question:
            LOGGER.warning("[%s] Empty question received.", question_id)
            return ComplianceOrchestrationResponse(
                question_id=question_id,
                error="No question was provided in the request.",
                stages=["validation:empty_question"],
            )

        LOGGER.info("[%s] Invoking NL2SQL LangGraph for: %r", question_id, question)

        initial_state: AgentState = AgentState(  # type: ignore[misc]
            question=question,
            question_id=question_id,
            stages=[],
        )

        try:
            final_state: AgentState = self._graph.invoke(initial_state)
        except ComplianceBotError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
            LOGGER.exception("[%s] Unexpected graph error: %s", question_id, exc)
            return ComplianceOrchestrationResponse(
                question_id=question_id,
                error=f"Unexpected pipeline error: {exc}",
                stages=["graph:unexpected_error"],
            )

        return self._map_state_to_response(question_id, final_state)

    # Maps the final AgentState to the outbound Pydantic response model.

    @staticmethod
    def _map_state_to_response(
        question_id: str,
        state: AgentState,
    ) -> ComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
        """
        Convert the final ``AgentState`` into a ``ComplianceOrchestrationResponse``.

        Args:
            question_id: Caller-supplied or auto-generated request identifier.
            state:       Final state returned by the LangGraph pipeline.

        Returns:
            Populated ``ComplianceOrchestrationResponse``.
        """
        clarification = state.get("clarification_needed")
        needs_clarification = bool(clarification)

        sql_query: str | None = state.get("sql_query") or None
        result_rows = state.get("result_rows") or None
        result_columns = list(state.get("result_columns") or [])
        result_summary: str | None = state.get("result_summary") or None
        error = state.get("error") or None
        stages = list(state.get("stages", []))
        interpreted = state.get("interpreted_question") or state.get("question", "")

        LOGGER.info(
            "[%s] Pipeline complete — stages=%s rows=%s error=%s",
            question_id,
            stages,
            len(result_rows) if result_rows else 0,
            error,
        )

        return ComplianceOrchestrationResponse(
            question_id=question_id,
            interpreted_question=interpreted,
            needs_clarification=needs_clarification,
            clarification_question=clarification,
            sql_query=sql_query,
            df_output=result_rows,
            result_columns=result_columns,
            result_summary=result_summary,
            stages=stages,
            error=error,
        )



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\exceptions\__init__.py
####################################################################################################

"""Compliance Chatbot — exceptions package."""



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\exceptions\custom_exceptions.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Custom exception hierarchy for the Compliance Chatbot application.

Provides structured, domain-specific exceptions that carry meaningful
error messages while preventing internal stack-trace leakage to clients.
"""


class ComplianceBotError(Exception):
    """
    Base exception for all Compliance Chatbot domain errors.

    All application-specific exceptions must inherit from this class
    so callers can catch them at a single abstraction level if required.
    """

    def __init__(self, message: str, detail: str | None = None) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the base exception.

        Args:
            message: Human-readable error summary.
            detail:  Optional additional technical detail (never surfaced to end-users).
        """
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message: str = message
        self.detail: str | None = detail

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.message


# Exceptions raised when the LLM provider fails to load or infer.


class ModelLoadError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an LLM or tokenizer fails to load."""


class ModelInferenceError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when the model produces an unexpected output during inference."""


class LLMProviderError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an LLM provider call fails (auth, HTTP error, bad response, etc.)."""


class LLMTimeoutError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an LLM provider call exceeds the configured timeout."""


class UnknownProviderError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an unrecognised LLM provider name is requested."""


# Exceptions raised during SQL generation or execution.


class SqlGenerationError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when the NL-to-SQL pipeline cannot produce a valid SQL statement."""


class SqlExecutionError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when a generated SQL query fails to execute against the database."""


# Exceptions raised on database connection or missing-record errors.


class DatabaseConnectionError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when the application cannot connect to the database."""


class RecordNotFoundError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an expected database record is absent."""


# Exceptions raised when raw data cannot be ingested or validated.


class DataIngestionError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when raw data cannot be ingested or structured correctly."""


class SchemaValidationError(ComplianceBotError):
    """Raised when an incoming payload fails schema validation."""

####################################################################################################
