"""Compliance Chatbot — services package."""



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\services\model_loader_service.py
####################################################################################################

"""
LLM provider service for the Compliance Chatbot.

Supports DLH self-hosted OpenAI-compatible inference and IBM Consulting
Advantage (ICA) OpenAI-compatible namespaces while exposing a consistent
``generate`` and ``extract_sql`` interface to the rest of the application.
"""

import re
from typing import Optional

import httpx
from openai import APIError, APITimeoutError, OpenAI

from config.settings import ModelSettings
from src.exceptions.custom_exceptions import ModelInferenceError, ModelLoadError
from src.utils.constants import DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS, DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE, SQL_BEGIN_TAG, SQL_END_TAG
from src.utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.services.model_loader")

_ICA_NAMESPACE_PATHS: dict[str, str] = {
    "agents": "/ica/v1/agents/",
    "assistants": "/ica/v1/assistants/",
    "chat-models": "/ica/v1/chat-models/",
    "digital-workforce": "/ica/v1/digital-workforce/",
}


class ModelLoaderService:
    """Manages the lifecycle of the configured remote LLM provider."""

    def __init__(self, settings: ModelSettings) -> None:
        self.settings: ModelSettings = settings
        self._client: Optional[OpenAI] = None
        self._send_sampling_params = True

    def load(self) -> None:
        """Load the configured provider resources."""
        provider = self.settings.provider.lower()
        if provider == "dlh":
            self._load_dlh_client()
            return
        if provider == "ica":
            self._load_ica_client()
            return
        raise ModelLoadError("Unsupported provider. Choose 'dlh' or 'ica'.")

    def _load_dlh_client(self) -> None:
        """Initialise the DLH OpenAI-compatible client."""
        http_client = None
        if not self.settings.dlh_verify_ssl:
            http_client = httpx.Client(verify=False)

        try:
            self._client = OpenAI(
                api_key=self.settings.dlh_api_key,
                base_url=self.settings.dlh_base_url,
                timeout=30.0,
                **({"http_client": http_client} if http_client is not None else {}),
            )
            LOGGER.info("DLH client initialised successfully.")
        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
            raise ModelLoadError(
                f"Failed to initialise DLH client: {exc}",
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

    def _load_ica_client(self) -> None:
        """Initialise the ICA OpenAI-compatible client."""
        if not self.settings.ica_api_key:
            raise ModelLoadError("ICA API key is required.")

        namespace = self.settings.ica_namespace.strip().lower()
        if namespace not in _ICA_NAMESPACE_PATHS:
            raise ModelLoadError(
                f"Unknown ICA namespace '{namespace}'. Choose from: {', '.join(_ICA_NAMESPACE_PATHS)}"
            )

        raw_host = self.settings.ica_base_url.rstrip("/")
        host = raw_host[:-4] if raw_host.endswith("/ica") else raw_host
        base_url = f"{host}{_ICA_NAMESPACE_PATHS[namespace]}"

        try:
            self._client = OpenAI(
                api_key=self.settings.ica_api_key,
                base_url=base_url,
                timeout=30.0,
                default_headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.settings.ica_api_key}"},
            )
            self._send_sampling_params = self.settings.ica_send_temperature
            LOGGER.info("ICA client initialised successfully.")
        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
            raise ModelLoadError(
                f"Failed to initialise ICA client: {exc}",
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

    def is_loaded(self) -> bool:
        """Return ``True`` when the configured provider is ready for inference."""
        return self._client is not None

    def test_connection(self) -> None:
        """Verify the configured remote provider connection with a minimal request."""
        provider = self.settings.provider.lower()
        if self._client is None:
            raise ModelLoadError("Remote client is not initialised.")

        request_kwargs = {
            "model": self.settings.model_name,
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "Reply with OK."}],
        }
        if provider == "dlh" or self._send_sampling_params:
            request_kwargs["temperature"] = 0.1
            request_kwargs["max_tokens"] = 8

        try:
            self._client.chat.completions.create(**request_kwargs)
        except APITimeoutError as exc:
            raise ModelInferenceError(f"Connection test timed out: {exc}", detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except APIError as exc:
            raise ModelInferenceError(f"Connection test failed: {exc}", detail=str(exc)) from exc

    def generate(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        max_new_tokens: int = DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS,
        temperature: float = DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE,
    ) -> str:
        """Run inference on the configured provider."""
        if not self.is_loaded():
            raise ModelInferenceError("Model is not loaded. Call ModelLoaderService.load() before generating.")

        request_kwargs = {
            "model": self.settings.model_name,
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        }
        if self.settings.provider.lower() == "dlh" or self._send_sampling_params:
            request_kwargs["temperature"] = temperature
            request_kwargs["max_tokens"] = max_new_tokens

        try:
            response = self._client.chat.completions.create(**request_kwargs)
            return response.choices[0].message.content or ""
        except APITimeoutError as exc:
            raise ModelInferenceError(f"Provider request timed out: {exc}", detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except APIError as exc:
            raise ModelInferenceError(f"Provider API error: {exc}", detail=str(exc)) from exc

    def extract_sql(self, model_output: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Extract the SQL statement from delimited model output."""
        pattern = re.compile(
            rf"{re.escape(SQL_BEGIN_TAG)}\s*(.*?)\s*{re.escape(SQL_END_TAG)}",
            re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE,
        )
        match = pattern.search(model_output)
        if match:
            sql = match.group(1).strip()
            LOGGER.debug("Extracted SQL: %s", sql)
            return sql

        LOGGER.warning("SQL delimiter tags not found in model output. Raw output: %s", model_output[:300])
        return None

    def unload(self) -> None:
        """Release provider resources from memory."""
        LOGGER.info("Unloading model resources from memory …")
        self._client = None
        LOGGER.info("Model unloaded.")

"""
Application-wide constants for the Compliance Chatbot.

All literal values that are used across more than one module must be
centralised here to satisfy the DRY principle.
"""

# Delimiter tags the LLM must wrap the generated SQL between.
SQL_BEGIN_TAG: str = "<begin_sql_response>"
SQL_END_TAG: str = "<end_sql_response>"

# Table and placeholder names used across the pipeline.
COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str = "COMP_TBL"
BRANCH_CODE_PLACEHOLDER: str = "{branch_code}"

# Key names used in API response dictionaries.
RESPONSE_QUESTION_ID: str = "question_id"
RESPONSE_SQL_QUERY: str = "sql_query"
RESPONSE_DF_OUTPUT: str = "df_output"

# Root logger name shared by all modules.
LOGGER_ROOT_NAME: str = "compliance_bot"

# Default token limit, temperature and stop sequences for LLM calls.
DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS: int = 512
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE: float = 0.1
DEFAULT_STOP_SEQUENCES: list[str] = [SQL_END_TAG]



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\utils\logger.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Centralised logging factory for the Compliance Chatbot application.

All modules must obtain their logger exclusively through :func:`get_logger`
to guarantee consistent formatting and handler configuration.
"""

import logging
import logging.config
import logging.handlers
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import yaml


_LOGGING_CONFIGURED: bool = False


def _configure_logging(log_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """
    Bootstrap logging from the YAML configuration file.

    Falls back to a minimal ``basicConfig`` if the YAML file is missing.

    Args:
        log_dir: Optional override for the log output directory.
    """
    global _LOGGING_CONFIGURED  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    if _LOGGING_CONFIGURED:
        return

    config_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "config" / "logging.yaml"

    # Ensure the log directory exists before loading config
    resolved_log_dir = Path(log_dir) if log_dir else Path("logs")
    resolved_log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if config_path.exists():
        with config_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            log_config = yaml.safe_load(fh)

        # Patch the rotating_file handler filename if a custom log_dir was given
        if log_dir and "handlers" in log_config:
            rotating = log_config["handlers"].get("rotating_file")
            if rotating:
                rotating["filename"] = str(resolved_log_dir / "compliance_bot.log")

        logging.config.dictConfig(log_config)
    else:
        logging.basicConfig(
            level=logging.INFO,
            format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
        )
        logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
            "YAML logging config not found at %s — using basicConfig fallback.",
            config_path,
        )

    _LOGGING_CONFIGURED = True


def get_logger(name: str, log_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> logging.Logger:
    """
    Return a named logger, ensuring logging is configured beforehand.

    Args:
        name:    Dot-separated logger name (e.g. ``"compliance_bot.services.nlsql"``).
        log_dir: Optional log output directory; used only on first call.

    Returns:
        Configured :class:`logging.Logger` instance.
    """
    _configure_logging(log_dir=log_dir)
    return logging.getLogger(name)
